# Vidra User Ralph Fix Plan

> Repo target: `vidra-user` only.
> Ralph must not modify `vidra-core` from this repo. Backend tasks belong in the backend repository and are tracked here only as API contract dependencies.

## ✅ FINAL RECONCILIATION — Completeness Campaign Close-Out (2026-07-03)

This ledger has been reconciled against the shipped code, a green `npm run ci`, and an
**executed** backend-backed suite:

- **`npm run ci` is green**: typecheck + lint + **384 unit/component** tests + `next build`
  + **350 mocked Playwright** specs. This is the canonical local↔CI gate — `frontend-ci.yml`
  runs exactly `npm run ci`, enforced by `ci-guard.yml`.
- **Backend-backed Playwright suite: 74 passed / 0 failed** against a real `vidra-core`
  + PostgreSQL (docker-compose `core` profile; `frontend-e2e-backed.yml` / `npm run e2e:backed`).
  This EXECUTES the specs that earlier item notes below describe as "written, runs with the
  backed suite, not executed this loop" — they are now **run and passing**, so the
  data-mutating flows they cover are **VERIFIED** per the Critical Verification Rule (real DB
  effect proven + reflected in the UI after refetch).
- **Env-gated backed specs NOT part of the 74** (they need a special stack or external
  provider the standard backed run does not provision, so they stay WRITTEN-and-gated, not
  VERIFIED): `e2e-backed/atproto.spec.ts` (live Bluesky PDS, `E2E_ATPROTO`),
  `e2e-backed/whisper-captions.spec.ts` (`WHISPER_ENABLED` stack), `e2e-backed/peertube-import.spec.ts`
  (read-only PeerTube source, `E2E_PEERTUBE_SOURCE`), `e2e-backed/registration-approval.spec.ts`
  (`REGISTRATION_REQUIRE_APPROVAL=true` stack), and `e2e-backed/quarantine.spec.ts`
  (`QUARANTINE_NEW_UPLOADS=true` stack — skips honestly otherwise).
- **Intentional differences from PeerTube** (kept honestly marked, not "missing"):
  torrent/magnet import (Vidra uses file/URL/live ingest + an IPFS/S3/HLS media architecture —
  the torrent tab is intentionally absent), DM typing-presence indicators, and free-form
  creator "Support" text (Vidra ships structured non-custodial crypto Donate instead).

Item notes below are kept for provenance; where a note still says a backed spec is
"written / not executed this loop", read it through this banner — it has since been executed
and passes, unless it is one of the five env-gated specs listed above.

## 🔴 Critical Verification Rule: Real Database Effects

A frontend feature that talks to the backend is **NOT done** until Ralph has proven
the change actually reached the database AND is reflected back in the UI. Mocked
API responses are acceptable for UI scaffolding only — never as completion evidence
for a feature that mutates real data.

For any feature that creates, updates, or deletes data, Ralph must demonstrate the
full round trip:

1. Perform the action in the running frontend (real `vidra-core` backend, not mocks).
2. Confirm the backend persisted it (query the database directly, or via a backend
   read endpoint) and capture that evidence.
3. Confirm the change is visible in the frontend after a fresh load/refetch
   (not just optimistic local state).
4. Capture evidence: a Playwright trace/screenshot showing the UI state plus the
   database row / API read confirming persistence.

If `vidra-core` does not yet expose the contract needed to prove persistence, mark
the item `BLOCKED` on the backend dependency — do not mark it `VERIFIED` on mocks.

## Operating Rules

- [ ] Before every loop, read `.ralph/PROMPT.md`, this `fix_plan.md`, `.ralph/AGENT.md`, and all files in `.ralph/specs/`.
- [ ] Work on one coherent vertical slice per loop.
- [ ] Search the codebase before adding routes, components, state, API clients, tests, or config.
- [ ] Keep PeerTube parity evidence current: feature ledger, UI/control inventory, route inventory, acceptance notes, and test evidence.
- [ ] Never mark a route/control `VERIFIED` without evidence: component tests, Playwright tests, screenshots/logs, backend contract, or manual QA notes.
- [ ] For any data-mutating feature, `VERIFIED` requires proof the change hit the real database AND is visible in the frontend after refetch (see Critical Verification Rule above). Mocks alone never satisfy `VERIFIED`.
- [ ] Never set `EXIT_SIGNAL: true` until every in-scope parity item and Vidra extension is `VERIFIED`, `INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE`, or explicitly deferred by the user.
- [ ] Keep commits small and descriptive.
- [ ] Do not store secrets, production credentials, OAuth secrets, stream keys, or wallet private keys in the repo.
- [ ] Do not copy PeerTube source code, assets, branding, screenshots, or exact styling. Use PeerTube only as behavioral reference.
- [ ] Avoid UI frameworks. Use custom components, Tailwind CSS, and minified inline/local SVG icons unless a dependency is clearly justified.

## Definition of Done for Any UI Feature

- [ ] Requirement is listed in the correct ledger.
- [ ] Route and controls are listed in `.ralph/specs/peertube-ui-inventory.md`.
- [ ] API contract dependency is documented.
- [ ] Loading, empty, success, error, and permission states are implemented.
- [ ] Keyboard and screen-reader behavior is considered.
- [ ] Responsive behavior is implemented.
- [ ] Component/unit tests cover logic.
- [ ] Integration tests cover API-client behavior.
- [ ] Playwright smoke/e2e tests cover critical path.
- [ ] For data-mutating features: end-to-end test against the real `vidra-core` backend proves the database changed and the change is visible in the UI after refetch (database/API read evidence + Playwright trace captured).
- [ ] `.ralph/fix_plan.md`, relevant ledger rows, and `.ralph/AGENT.md` are updated.
- [ ] Focused checks pass locally or the failure is documented as a blocker.

- [x] Initialize or verify Next.js app. (Next 16 app router; scaffolded via create-next-app then adapted; `npm run dev|build|start`.)
- [x] Verify strict TypeScript configuration. (`tsconfig.json` `strict: true`; `npm run typecheck` = `tsc --noEmit`, green.)
- [x] Configure Tailwind CSS. (Tailwind v4 via `@tailwindcss/postcss`; `app/globals.css` `@import "tailwindcss"`.)
- [x] Add path aliases. (`@/*` → project root.)

- [ ] Add Prettier or formatting command if desired.
- [x] Add app directory routing conventions. (`app/layout.tsx` + `app/page.tsx`.)
- [x] Add global styles and design tokens. (Slice `user-primitives`, 2026-07-03: `app/globals.css` now defines a **semantic color scale** — surface/surface-muted/surface-raised, fg/fg-muted/fg-subtle, border/border-subtle, accent/accent-fg, focus, danger/danger-fg/danger-surface/danger-border, success, warning — as raw `:root` vars with a `prefers-color-scheme: dark` override, exposed to Tailwind via `@theme inline` so `bg-surface`/`text-fg`/`border-border`/`bg-accent text-accent-fg`/`text-danger` etc. flip in dark mode automatically (no `dark:` on the primitives). Values mirror the zinc/red palette the app was hand-writing, so token adoption is a visual no-op. Spacing/radius/typography stay on Tailwind's default scale (documented). A global `prefers-reduced-motion` reset neutralizes primitive transitions/animations. Documented in `.ralph/specs/design-system.md`.)
- [x] Add custom component primitives: Button, LinkButton, IconButton, Input, Textarea, Select, Checkbox, Radio, Toggle, Modal, Dropdown, Tabs, Toast, Card, Badge, Avatar, Skeleton, EmptyState, ErrorState. (Slice `user-primitives`, 2026-07-03: the full set lives in `components/ui/` (barrel `@/components/ui`), token-driven + accessible, no UI framework. `Button` (variants primary/secondary/danger/ghost × sm/md/lg, shares `buttonClasses` with `LinkButton`), `IconButton` (mandatory `label`), `Input`/`Textarea`/`Select` (label+hint+error, auto `useId`, aria-invalid/describedby), `Checkbox`/`Radio` (native, label-wrapped), `Toggle` (`role="switch"`+aria-checked), `Modal` (`role="dialog" aria-modal`, focus-trap + focus restore, Escape + backdrop close), `Dropdown` (menu-button pattern: aria-haspopup/expanded, Arrow/Home/End/Escape, outside-click), `Tabs` (WAI-ARIA roving tabindex, Arrow/Home/End), `Toast`+`ToastProvider` (`useToast()`, polite status / assertive alert, auto-dismiss), `Card`/`Badge`/`Skeleton`; the pre-existing `Avatar`/`Spinner`/`EmptyState`/`ErrorState`/`LoadMoreButton` are kept (dir extended, not duplicated). Proven by refactoring the auth forms (`LoginForm`/`SignupForm` → Input/Button), the report modal (`ReportButton` → Modal/Textarea/Button), and an admin surface (`AdminOverview` → Card/Badge) onto them — all existing e2e selectors stayed green (318 mocked specs). RTL component-unit tests cover Button/Modal/Tabs/Toast/Select (see P14). Documented in `.ralph/specs/design-system.md`.)

## P1.2 Configuration and API Client

- [x] Add `.env.example`. (`NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL`, `LOG_LEVEL`, `OTEL_ENABLED`.)
- [x] Add `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL`. (in `.env.example`; the typed config module that reads it is a follow-up.)
- [~] Add server-side API base URL option if needed. (one `apiBaseUrl` from `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL` works server + client; a separate internal URL can be added if SSR needs it.)
- [x] Add typed config module. (`lib/config.ts` — `apiBaseUrl` (trailing-slash trimmed), `otelEnabled`.)
- [x] Add API client foundation. (`lib/api/client.ts` `apiRequest<T>` fetch wrapper + typed `api.*` endpoint fns in `lib/api/endpoints.ts`: instance, feed (sort/page), video detail, search, channel, channel videos; plus `videoOriginalUrl`/`videoThumbnailUrl` helpers. Vitest-covered with mocked fetch — 16 tests.)
- [x] Add auth token storage strategy. (Access token: in-memory only (`lib/api/auth-store.ts`), auto-attached by the client, never localStorage/sessionStorage per the observability spec, never logged. Refresh token: httpOnly `vidra_refresh` cookie the JS never sees — login/register send `cookie_mode: true` with `credentials: "include"`; ONLY the session endpoints (register/login/refresh/logout) send cookies, every other call stays cookie-free.)
- [x] Add refresh/session handling strategy. (Hard reloads: `AuthProvider` boots with ONE silent cookie-mode `POST /auth/refresh` (no body token) — success stores the rotated access token in memory + loads `/auth/me`; failure lands quietly signed out (new "restoring" session status: header `AccountMenu` shows a placeholder, `SettingsView` a spinner, no error UI). Token expiry: a 401 on a stored-token call triggers one single-flight silent refresh + one retry with the rotated token (`restoreSession`/retry in `lib/api/client.ts`, `retryOn401: false` on the session endpoints); a failed refresh or a second 401 signs out app-wide via the auth-store session-expired handler. Unit-tested (`client.test.ts` restoreSession + 401-retry suites, `auth.test.ts` cookie-mode bodies/credentials — 32 green); mocked `e2e/session.spec.ts` (reload keeps session; refresh-failure → signed out, no error UI; 401 → one refresh → retry; double-401 → sign-out; logout `{}` body + reload stays signed out — 5 green). Backed proof WRITTEN in `e2e-backed/session.spec.ts` (signup → hard reload ×2 stays signed in via rotation → sign out → reload stays signed out); runs with the backed suite/CI, not yet executed in this loop.)
- [x] Add standardized error mapping. (`ApiError` maps the `{error:{code,message,request_id,fields}}` envelope; non-envelope → generic `http_error`; network failure → `network_error`.)
- [x] Add request ID propagation if backend supports it. (every call sends `X-Correlation-ID`, which vidra-core accepts/echoes; W3C `traceparent` lands with the OTel instrumentation slice.)
- [x] Add generated or hand-maintained API types until backend OpenAPI exists. **Superseded by real OpenAPI→TS codegen** (slice `user-typegen`, 2026-07-03, satisfies vidra-core fix_plan P3.1). `scripts/codegen.mjs` (npm script `codegen`) runs the pinned `openapi-typescript@^7.13.0` against `../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml` (spec resolution mirrors `check-contract.mjs`: `$OPENAPI_PATH` → sibling → legacy) and writes `lib/api/generated.ts` (committed, eslint-ignored, 266 schemas). `lib/api/types.ts` no longer hand-copies shapes: all 167 exported names are now thin aliases over the generated `components["schemas"]`/`operations` (e.g. `export type Video = Schemas["Video"]`; enums via field access wrapped in `NonNullable`, e.g. `VideoState = NonNullable<Schemas["Video"]["state"]>`; the 3 inline reset/verify bodies via `operations[...]["requestBody"]`). Every consumer still imports from `@/lib/api` unchanged. Regenerating against the CURRENT spec surfaced real drift the hand-written mirror had masked (login/register `cookie_mode` now required — accepted omitted at the `authApi`/`useSession` boundary since the client always injects it; `Video.privacy`/`state`/`duration_seconds` now optional/nullable for remote cards — coalesced at the studio/watch sites that only ever see local videos; `UploadVideoFileResponse.file` and live-stream defaulted fields now required — test fixtures completed). `npm run ci` green (typecheck proves every alias resolves + shape-compatibility: 340 unit + 318 e2e).)
- [x] Mark all provisional API types as pending backend contract. **Resolved**: types are no longer provisional/hand-maintained — they are DERIVED from the spec, so backend shape drift now breaks `tsc` at the call sites. `contract-ci.yml` gained a freshness gate (`npm ci` → `npm run codegen` from the public vidra-core spec → `git diff --quiet lib/api/generated.ts`), upgrading the contract check from path-presence-only to full shape-drift detection.

(`app/reset-password/confirm/page.tsx` reads the `token` query param (server component) → `components/auth/ResetPasswordConfirmForm.tsx`: new-password + confirm-password → `authApi.confirmPasswordReset` (`POST /auth/password-reset/confirm`, 204). A client-side mismatch is caught before submitting; a 400 (invalid/used/expired) or a missing token shows an invalid-link state linking back to `/reset-password`; a 422 maps the `password` field error inline; success shows a confirmation + a Sign in link (the backend revokes all sessions). Login is MFA-aware: `authApi.login` is now typed `AuthResponse | MFARequiredResponse` and `useSession().login` returns a `LoginOutcome` — on `{mfa_required, mfa_token}` the login form SWAPS to a code-entry step (heading "Two-factor authentication", `Authentication code` input with `autocomplete=one-time-code`, help copy covering TOTP *or* recovery code, "Back to sign in" reset) and completes via new `useSession().completeMfaChallenge` → `POST /auth/mfa/challenge` `{mfa_token, code, cookie_mode:true}` (credentials include, `retryOn401:false` — a 401 is a real answer). Honest errors: 401 → "That code didn't work, or this sign-in attempt has expired…", 429 → "Too many attempts — wait a moment and try again." No session exists until the challenge passes (asserted). Verify: first valid code → `POST /auth/mfa/totp/verify` (400 → honest retry copy). Disable: password-confirmed `DELETE /auth/mfa/totp` danger zone (403 → "Incorrect password."). /settings gains a "Security" manage card. API client: `getAccountExport`/`requestAccountExport`/`downloadAccountExport`/`importAccountArchive` + `AccountExportStatus`/`AccountArchive`/`AccountImportSummary` types (unit-tested — auth.test.ts 28 green). (Slice `user-auth-ui`, 2026-07-03, consuming the landed `/auth/oauth/*` + `/me/oauth-identities` contract + `InstanceResponse.oauth_providers` (all verified in openapi.yaml; check-contract green, 120 paths / 105 referenced). **Buttons**: login AND signup render one "Continue with <Provider>" anchor per `GET /instance` `oauth_providers` entry (`components/auth/OAuthButtons.tsx`; plain `<a>` because the begin endpoint 302s to the provider — top-level navigation, never fetch; none configured → section hidden entirely). **Session handback (verified against the backend contract + `internal/httpapi/oauth.go`)**: the callback ALWAYS issues the session in cookie mode (httpOnly `vidra_refresh`) and 302s to the validated same-origin `return_to` — no tokens ever appear in query/fragment. The frontend therefore passes `return_to=/login?oauth=1` (resp. `/signup?oauth=1`) and on landing shows a "Completing sign-in" state while the boot silent-refresh (`restoreSession`) turns the cookie into a session → authed lands home; a settled anon is surfaced honestly ("Could not complete the sign-in with the provider."). User-actionable failures come back as `?oauth_error=<code>` → mapped to honest copy (`access_denied`/`email_conflict`/`email_required`/`account_disabled`/`conflict`). BOTH one-shot markers are cleaned from the URL immediately (`router.replace`). NOTE: the relative `return_to` redirect resolves against the backend's PUBLIC_BASE_URL origin — production must serve SPA+API from one canonical origin (already the deployment stance); dev/e2e same-site localhost works because the refresh cookie is SameSite=Lax same-site across ports. **Connected logins**: /settings section (`components/auth/ConnectedLogins.tsx`) lists `GET /me/oauth-identities` (provider, email snapshot, linked date; loading/error+retry/empty states) with per-row Unlink (aria-label "Unlink <Provider>") → `DELETE /me/oauth-identities/{provider}`; the last-credential **422 is surfaced** with the remedy ("Set a password first…"), 404 treated as already-unlinked. API client: `oauthBeginUrl` helper + `listOAuthIdentities`/`unlinkOAuthIdentity` + `OAuthIdentity(iesResponse)`/`MFARequiredResponse` types (unit-tested). Mocked `e2e/oauth.spec.ts` (10: per-provider buttons + hrefs incl. return_to on login AND signup; none-configured hides section; oauth_error landing shows copy + cleans URL (login + signup variants); cookie-mode success landing silent-refreshes → home signed-in; silent-failure falls back to form; connected-logins list + unlink row removal + 422 remedy + empty copy). Backed: `e2e-backed/oauth-identities.spec.ts` WRITTEN (read-only wire-up: section loads empty against the real backend); the full link/unlink round trip is **provider-gated** — the backed compose stack has no OIDC provider; recorded here as the remaining OAuth verification dependency (needs a fake-OIDC service in the backed stack).)

Evidence captured: the UI-created row in Postgres (`psql … users WHERE email LIKE 'e2e-%'`) + a Playwright trace (`trace: "on"`). **Profile-edit also VERIFIED**: `e2e-backed/profile-edit.spec.ts` signs up → edits the display name on the settings page → saves → signs out → signs back in → the new display name is still shown (a fresh DB round-trip); DB evidence captured (`psql … display_name`). The profile is intentionally NOT part of `npm run ci` (kept mocked/fast); see `.ralph/AGENT.md` for the run procedure. **Deactivate also VERIFIED**: `e2e-backed/deactivate.spec.ts` signs up → deactivates (password confirm) → a fresh login with the same credentials is refused ("account is disabled"); DB evidence captured (`psql … is_active=f`). All backed specs use a `randomUUID` email/username so they're collision-free under parallel runs. **CI-automated**: the root `frontend-e2e-backed.yml` workflow stands up the compose `core` stack and runs `npm run e2e:backed` on every push/PR touching either project, so the backed coverage is enforced (not just local). The auth rate-limit headroom that previously capped the backed suite is RESOLVED: the compose api now honours `RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` (env-overridable, default true) and `frontend-e2e-backed.yml` + the local procedure set `RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED=false`, so the suite can grow without throttling (verified: 3 back-to-back local runs pass without a redis flush, where run 2 previously failed). The email-token flows are now UNBLOCKED: vidra-core ships a dev-only token seam (`GET /api/v1/dev/email-token`, gated by `DEV_MAIL_CAPTURE_ENABLED`, which the backed CI workflow enables). Both email-token flows are now VERIFIED against it: password-reset **confirm** (`e2e-backed/password-reset-confirm.spec.ts`) and email **verification** pending/resend + confirm (`e2e-backed/email-verify.spec.ts`, `kind=verification`).

## P4.2 Watch Page

- [x] Implement video watch route. (`app/videos/[id]/page.tsx` → `components/WatchView.tsx`, the destination feed cards link to.)
- [~] Implement custom video player wrapper. (native `<video controls playsInline>` with `has_thumbnail` poster; the stream is now HLS (hls.js/native) when the detail carries `hls_url`, else the Range-capable `videoOriginalUrl` — see the quality-selector item. A custom-controls wrapper (speed/captions menu/shortcuts) is a later slice; quality lives in the under-player `QualityMenu`.)
- [x] Implement play/pause. (native controls)
- [x] Implement timeline/seek. (native controls; backend serves HTTP Range so seeking works)
- [x] Implement volume/mute. (native controls)
- [x] Implement captions toggle. (The watch-page `Player` (`components/WatchView.tsx`) loads a video's caption tracks (`GET /videos/:id/captions`) and renders a `<track kind="captions">` per track inside the native `<video controls>`, which surfaces the browser CC toggle. Each WebVTT body is fetched and served via a same-origin blob URL (sidesteps the native cross-origin `<track>` restriction without adding `crossorigin` to the media element, so the Range stream is untouched); `videoCaptionUrl` helper added + unit-tested. Mocked `e2e/watch.spec.ts` asserts the `<track>` renders. **VERIFIED** end-to-end in `e2e-backed/captions-watch.spec.ts`: a published video with an uploaded WebVTT caption shows a `srclang=en`/blob-src `<track>` on the watch page — the cross-origin VTT fetch succeeds under the backend CORS (caption row confirmed via psql). A curated in-player caption *menu* with styling is a later custom-player slice.)
- [x] Implement quality selector. (HLS playback + quality menu, consuming the just-landed backend transcoding contract (`../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml`: detail-only `hls_url`/`renditions`, `GET /videos/:id/hls/master.m3u8` + `/hls/:rendition/:file`). `lib/use-hls-playback.ts` (shared by watch + embed): when the detail carries `hls_url`, play the ladder via **hls.js** over MSE (DEPENDENCY ADDED: `hls.js@1.6.16`, Apache-2.0, zero-dep, the standard MSE HLS client — **dynamically imported** so the chunk never loads for non-HLS/native playback); native `<video src>` HLS only when MSE is absent (iOS Safari) — hls.js preferred even where native HLS is also claimed (Playwright's Chromium claims both) because it is the only quality-controllable path; no `hls_url` → the existing Range-capable `/original` playback, unchanged. Fatal-error strategy: pre-manifest fatal (e.g. the playlist 404s despite the detail advertising it) → fall back to `/original`; post-manifest → bounded hls.js-recommended recovery (startLoad/recoverMediaError, ×2) then fall back — playback degrades instead of dying. `?t=` honoured via hls.js `startPosition` (media-fragment `#t=` for native/original). `components/QualityMenu.tsx`: accessible menu button ("Quality: Auto") → `role="menu"` of `menuitemradio` entries (Auto = level −1 + one per parsed rendition height, tallest first) driving `hls.currentLevel`; arrow-key cycling, Escape/outside-click close, focus returned to the button; hidden for native-HLS/original playback (nothing is controllable there). Pure helpers in `lib/hls.ts` (`choosePlaybackMode`, `buildLevelMenu`, `canPlayNativeHls`) unit-tested (`lib/hls.test.ts`, 10) + `videoHlsMasterUrl` (contract-checked, unit-tested). Mocked `e2e/hls.spec.ts` (5): REAL hls.js parses tiny valid m3u8 fixtures served via `page.route` (segments aborted — actual frames are not asserted under mock, honest scope) → blob: MediaSource src + menu entries/selection/aria-checked/Escape-focus; master-404 → `/original` fallback; MSE-deleted → native master src, no menu; no-`hls_url` → original src, no menu; embed → HLS at adaptive default, no menu. Backed spec WRITTEN (not run this slice — runs against the rebuilt stack): `e2e-backed/hls-playback.spec.ts` — publish → poll the detail until the async transcode persists `hls_url` (`waitForHls` fixture helper) → master/variant/segment endpoints serve the stored tree → the watch page streams via hls.js with the real rendition heights in the menu; `frontend-e2e-backed.yml` now starts the stack with `TRANSCODING_ENABLED=true` (the compose api image ships ffmpeg), and `e2e-backed/embed.spec.ts`'s src assertion widened to accept the HLS blob: src (the async transcode may finish before that page loads).)
- [x] Implement speed selector. (`components/SpeedMenu.tsx` on the under-player controls row, next to the quality menu: "Speed: Normal" menu button → 0.25×–2× `menuitemradio` entries driving the native `video.playbackRate` (+ `defaultPlaybackRate`, re-applied on src change since a media load resets the rate). Works on every playback path (hls.js / native HLS / original), so it is always shown. The QualityMenu's accessible menu-button pattern was extracted into a shared `components/PlayerMenu.tsx` (open-focuses-checked-item, arrow cycling, Escape/outside-click close + focus return) that both menus now wrap — hls.spec selectors unchanged. Mocked `e2e/watch-player.spec.ts` "speed selector" (menu a11y attrs + `playbackRate` 1.5 asserted on the element).)
- [x] Implement fullscreen. (native controls)
- [x] Implement keyboard shortcuts where specified. (Watch-page player shortcuts: space/K play-pause, J/L ±10s, ←/→ ±5s, M mute, F fullscreen, C captions toggle (all tracks off → first track on). Pure mapping + seek clamping in `lib/player-shortcuts.ts` (unit-tested — 8 tests incl. modifier/unmapped rejection and NaN-duration clamping); the DOM wiring (document keydown in `WatchView` `Player`) ignores presses while typing in / operating any interactive control (`input/textarea/select/button/a/[contenteditable]/[role=menu]/[role=dialog]`) and when the `<video>`'s own focused native controls would handle the key. Documented in an accessible disclosure (`components/KeyboardShortcutsHelp.tsx`: button `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls` → `role="region"` list, Escape closes + returns focus). (Fixes the publish **false-success bug**: the outcome is now driven by the returned `video.state`, not by the HTTP call succeeding — `published` → "Published!" + View link; `processing` → an honest amber "…is still processing" `role="status"` message (no Published!); `failed` (probe/scan rejected the file after a 201) → an inline "Processing failed — the file could not be published…" error (`role="alert"`), keeping the form values for a retry — identically on the URL-import path (import-specific wording via `failedStateError`). In-flight remains the "Uploading…/Importing…" button state; byte-level progress stays the P6.2 DEFERRED item. - [x] Implement cancellation UI. Cancellation rejects as a distinct `upload_cancelled` `ApiError` (`isUploadCancelled`), so it is never mistaken for a server error. Mocked `e2e/studio.spec.ts` "a slow upload shows a determinate progress bar and can be cancelled" (held-open route → progress bar + Cancel → editable form, values kept, no "Published!", draft DELETE asserted). Backed spec WRITTEN (pending a stack run): `e2e-backed/upload-cancel.spec.ts` — a CDP-throttled 16 MB upload is cancelled mid-flight, the draft DELETE is awaited, and the owner-authed channel list proves NO orphaned row persisted in PostgreSQL.)

## P6.2 File Upload

- [x] Implement file picker. (`UploadSection` `<input type="file" accept="video/*">`; on Publish it creates a draft (`api.createVideoDraft`) then uploads the file (`api.uploadVideoFile`, multipart via `apiRequest` FormData support) which the backend probes + publishes. 415 → "not a supported video" message. A drag-and-drop dropzone is a later polish.)
- [x] Implement upload progress. (Real byte-level progress: `lib/api/upload.ts` `uploadWithProgress(url, form, {token, onProgress, signal})` wraps XMLHttpRequest (fetch cannot observe request-body progress) and is used ONLY by `api.uploadVideoFile` — every other call stays on `apiRequest`/fetch. Error envelopes map through the shared `apiErrorFromBody` (extracted from client.ts), so 413/415/422-with-fields behave exactly like `apiRequest`'s ApiError and the publish flow's failed-state / inline-422 handling is unchanged. The publish form shows a determinate `role="progressbar"` (`aria-valuenow` 0–100, `aria-label` "Upload progress") + percent text while a file upload is in flight. Unit tests `lib/api/upload.test.ts` (mocked XHR: headers/no content-type, rounded percents, 413/422-fields/non-JSON/network/abort/pre-aborted mapping, empty-body resolve — 10 tests) + the `endpoints.test.ts` `uploadVideoFile` test rewritten for the XHR transport. The existing mocked published/processing/failed specs now exercise the XHR path end-to-end.)
- [~] Implement virus scan pending/quarantine/failure states. (**Quarantine states DONE** (the QUARANTINE_NEW_UPLOADS moderation-hold pipeline, distinct from ClamAV): the publish outcome treats a returned `state=quarantined` as its OWN amber "held for review — this instance reviews new uploads before they go public" `role="status"` message (never "Published!" and never a failure), and studio rows badge `quarantined` with explanatory copy ("Held for review … Only you and the moderators can see it until it is approved."). Mocked `e2e/quarantine.spec.ts` (outcome + row copy). ClamAV scanning pending/failure states remain DEFERRED — backend ClamAV not wired.)
- [~] Implement transcoding pending/progress/failure states. (The honest subset is now DONE; the rest stays a recorded backend contract dependency. Checked `../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml` (2026-07-02): the transcoding pipeline landed but the contract exposes exactly ONE signal — the DETAIL response carries `hls_url`+`renditions` once the ladder is ready and omits them while transcoding is pending, **failed, or disabled** (indistinguishable); there is no queue/progress/failure state, and list rows omit the field entirely. So: the studio per-video **edit surface** (which already fetches the detail) shows a `StreamingStatus` note — "Streaming (HLS) ready: 720p, 480p." when `hls_url` is present, or a hedged "HD streaming versions are not ready — viewers currently watch the original file. They appear automatically once transcoding completes (if enabled on this instance)." for published-without-`hls_url` — deliberately NOT a row badge (that would need an N+1 detail fetch per row, or a lie). Mocked `e2e/studio.spec.ts` (2 tests: ready-with-renditions, not-ready note). FOLLOW-UP (backend contract dependency): a per-video transcode state (queued/running/failed) + an hls-ready flag on list views for real row badges and failure surfacing.)
- [x] Implement resumable (chunked) upload. (Slice `user-publish-completeness`, 2026-07-03 — the studio file upload switched from the single multipart `POST /videos/:id/file` to the CHUNKED protocol landed in `../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml`: `POST /videos/:id/upload-session` (declare size+filename → fixed 8 MiB chunk_size + total_chunks + 24h expiry) → sequential `PUT /uploads/:id/chunks/:n` (raw octet-stream, **3× retry with exponential backoff** on transient network/5xx, fail-fast on 4xx) → `POST /uploads/:id/complete` (assemble + finalise through the same probe/scan/transcode pipeline). Progress is now CHUNK-ACCURATE — driven by the server's `bytes_received` after each chunk, not a single request-body stream. **Cancel** aborts the in-flight chunk and `DELETE /uploads/:id`s the session (dropping its chunk blobs) plus the orphaned draft. **Resume**: each opened session is remembered in `localStorage` (uploadId+videoId+filename+size+expiry); a mid-upload interruption (e.g. a browser refresh) leaves a resumable session, and re-picking the SAME file (matched by name+size) surfaces a "Resume upload" banner that `GET`s the session status and PUTs only the not-yet-received chunks before completing — honest chunk-exhausted failure keeps the session resumable. New `lib/api/resumable-upload.ts` (orchestrator + chunk transport + localStorage store) — unit-tested `lib/api/resumable-upload.test.ts` (9: fresh 3-chunk upload URLs/headers/complete, resume skips received chunks, transient-retry-then-succeed, retry-exhaustion leaves session resumable, 4xx fail-fast, pre-aborted cancel, store remember/find/forget/expiry) + `createUploadSession`/`getUploadSession`/`cancelUploadSession`/`completeUploadSession` on the client (unit-tested). Mocked `e2e/studio.spec.ts` rewritten to the chunked contract (upload+publish, tags, failed-state, processing, **slow chunk → progress bar + Cancel → session+draft DELETE**, **re-picked file → Resume banner → only missing chunks PUT → Published**); `studio-schedule.spec.ts` + `quarantine.spec.ts` upload cases updated to chunked. Backed proof WRITTEN (not run this loop): `e2e-backed/studio.spec.ts` "…(chunked upload)" waits on `/uploads/:id/complete`; `e2e-backed/upload-cancel.spec.ts` "a cancelled chunked upload leaves no session and no published video" (CDP-throttled 16 MB → Cancel → session DELETE + draft DELETE awaited → owner channel list has no row). The direct `POST /videos/:id/file` endpoint stays for API/fixture use. Backend: upload sessions/chunks (vidra-core migration 0059 + `internal/upload`).)
- [x] Implement success redirect. (On publish the form shows "Published!" with a link to the new video's watch page (`/videos/:id`); a hard auto-redirect is intentionally avoided so the creator can upload another. Now gated on the returned `video.state === "published"` — a failed/processing result no longer claims Published! (see the P6.1 upload/import status-states item).)
- [x] Add Playwright file-upload smoke test with small fixture/mocked API. (`e2e/studio.spec.ts` — anon prompt, create channel → upload form appears, upload (setInputFiles) → "Published!" + view link — 3 tests.)
- [x] Add backend-backed e2e proving an uploaded video and its edited metadata persist to the database and appear in the studio list after refetch. (**VERIFIED**: `e2e-backed/studio.spec.ts` — signup → studio → create channel → upload the tiny H.264 mp4 (real ffprobe accepts it) → "Published!" → the video appears on the public channel page after a fresh refetch. DB evidence: the `videos` row (`state=published`, `privacy=public`) + `video_files` (original + generated thumbnail) via psql; channel API returns it published. Runs in CI via `frontend-e2e-backed.yml`.)

The up-front 422 (bad/non-public URL, validated before enqueue) still maps inline onto the URL field. `importVideoFile` returns `ImportJobResponse` + new `getVideoImport` (unit-tested); `ImportJob`/`ImportJobResponse`/`ImportJobState` types added.)

API client `importVideoFile`/`getVideoImport` unit-tested. **Backed proof UPDATED** `e2e-backed/studio.spec.ts` "a creator can publish a video by importing from a URL (async job)": a public source video is seeded, imported by its OWN `/original` via the compose service name (`http://api:8080/…`, no external origin) — the POST 202 is awaited then the studio polls until the imported video appears published on the importer's channel. Requires the backend's `HTTP_IMPORT_ALLOW_PRIVATE_URLS=true` (set in `frontend-e2e-backed.yml`); the vidra-core Content-Type fallback accepts the extension-less `/original`. Backend: `POST /videos/:id/import` (vidra-core `internal/videoimport` async worker).)

The native `<video controls>` owns its own seekbar, so this adds a separate accessible scrubber (`components/StoryboardPreview.tsx`, `role="slider"` "Seek preview", keyboard: arrows/Home/End/Enter): it fetches+parses the VTT (`lib/storyboard.ts` pure `parseStoryboardVtt`/`cueAt`/`parseVttTimestamp` — unit-tested `lib/storyboard.test.ts`, 11), and on pointer-hover/focus renders the sprite frame under the cursor (background-image of `videoStoryboardImageUrl` positioned by the cue's `#xywh`), seeking the player on click/Enter. Duration comes from the media element (tracked in state) else the detail's `duration_seconds` else the last cue. `Video.has_storyboard` type + `videoStoryboardVttUrl`/`videoStoryboardImageUrl` helpers (contract-checked, unit-tested). Mocked `e2e/watch-player.spec.ts` (2: has_storyboard → slider visible, hover → sprite preview, click → seek recorded; no storyboard → no scrubber). Read-only rendering of backend-generated media — no data mutation, so no backed spec. Backend: storyboard generation (vidra-core P6.3).)

## P6.4 Torrent/Magnet Import — INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE

> **INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE from PeerTube.** PeerTube behavior: a "Import with torrent"
> publish tab accepting a `.torrent` file or a magnet URI. **Vidra behavior:** no torrent/
> magnet import tab — the publish surface offers file upload, URL import, and live only.
> **Reason:** Vidra's media architecture is IPFS/S3/HLS (see P15 Optional item
> "WebTorrent/P2P playback if intentionally replaced by IPFS/S3/HLS architecture"); the
> backend ships no torrent importer contract, so a torrent tab would be a dead control.
> **User/admin/operator impact:** creators import via a public direct URL or file upload
> instead; no P2P seeding/leeching surface. **Security/privacy/federation impact:** avoids
> the torrent/magnet parser + tracker/DHT attack surface entirely. **Tests proving Vidra
> behavior:** the publish flow's Source toggle exposes only Upload-file / Import-from-URL
> (`e2e/studio.spec.ts`); no torrent control exists to test. Tracked as a documented
> divergence, not a gap.

- [x] Implement torrent file picker. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — n/a, no torrent tab.)
- [x] Implement magnet URI input. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — n/a, no torrent tab.)
- [x] Implement rights/legal warning text. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — n/a, no torrent tab; the URL importer carries its own expectations copy.)
- [x] Implement import submit/progress/error states. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — n/a, no torrent importer; file/URL import submit/progress/error states are covered in P6.2/P6.3.)

## P8.2 Encrypted Messaging

> Slice `user-e2ee-ui` (2026-07-03) — implements the E2EE DM UX per
> `../vidra-core/.ralph/specs/e2ee.md §5` against the committed `/api/v1/e2ee/*`
> contract. All cryptography is client-side via **@matrix-org/olm** WASM (the one
> allowed dependency), loaded at RUNTIME via a `<script>` from `/olm/` (Turbopack
> cannot bundle its emscripten glue; `scripts/copy-olm-wasm.mjs` `prebuild`-copies
> it into `public/olm/`, gitignored). The Olm account is pickled with a random
> key in IndexedDB (`lib/e2ee/store.ts`), device-bound by design. The crypto is
> injected through a seam (`getCryptoProvider` → `window.__VIDRA_E2EE_CRYPTO__`)
> so mocked runs stub it and never touch WASM. NB: the real Olm path
> (`lib/e2ee/olm-crypto.ts`) is exercised ONLY by the write-only backed spec — it
> is not run in `npm run ci` (mocked + build only).

- [x] Display encrypted mode availability only when backend contract supports it. (No-pretending rule: `lib/e2ee/availability.ts` probes `GET /api/v1/e2ee/devices` once per session; `StartEncryptedButton` renders **nothing** until the probe resolves non-404 (contract present). A 404 (older backend) or anon → hidden. Mocked `e2e/e2ee.spec.ts` proves both: "Encrypted message" appears on a comment when advertised, and is absent when `GET /e2ee/devices` 404s.)
- [x] Implement device setup/onboarding UI. (`components/e2ee/DeviceSetup.tsx` — first-encrypted-use flow: name this browser → `E2EEEngine.setupDevice` creates the Olm account, `POST /e2ee/devices` (public identity+signing keys), uploads a batch of one-time prekeys (`POST …/one-time-keys`), pickles into IndexedDB. `EncryptedThreadView` runs it automatically when no device exists. OTK replenishment ≥20 maintained: `replenishOneTimeKeys` tops back up to 30 whenever `GET …/count` < 20 (unit-tested threshold in `lib/e2ee/engine.test.ts`). Also a **Settings → Devices** page (`app/settings/devices` → `components/e2ee/DevicesView.tsx`, linked from `/settings`): list devices (`GET /e2ee/devices`) + delete (`DELETE /e2ee/devices/:id`, two-step confirm, cascades envelopes) + per-device safety number. No rename control — the contract has none.)
- [x] Implement encrypted conversation indicator. (Lock everywhere: the inbox row (`MessagesView`) shows a lock + "Encrypted conversation" (preview always empty — server can't read it) and links with `?to={peer}`; the thread header (`ConversationView`) reads "Encrypted conversation"; `EncryptedThreadView`'s `LockHeader` shows an "End-to-end encrypted" banner. `ConversationView` branches plaintext vs encrypted on the messages response shape (`"messages"` vs `"envelopes"`), so a direct load needs no prior type knowledge.)
- [x] Implement disappearing message timer control. (Composer `<select>` "Disappearing messages timer": off/1h/1d/1w → `expires_in_seconds` (pure `expiresInSeconds` helper, unit-tested, all within the backend 30s–90d bound) on the `SendEncryptedMessageRequest`. Mocked `e2e/e2ee.spec.ts` selects "1 day" and asserts the POST body carries `expires_in_seconds: 86400`.)
- [~] Implement ciphertext-safe attachment warnings where needed. (N/A this slice — E2EE attachments are DEFERRED with plaintext DM attachments (no backend contract). Instead the honest §1 limitation copy ships: the lock header states metadata is visible and a new device can't read earlier messages; the device/safety-number panels explain the server could substitute keys for an unverified peer.)
- [x] Do not pretend E2EE exists unless backend storage/protocol evidence exists. (See availability probe above — the affordance is contract-gated; there is no fake/local-only encryption path.)
- [x] Add tests for encrypted-mode UI states. (**Unit**: pure fan-out/timer/safety-number helpers (`lib/e2ee/envelope.test.ts`, 9), engine setup/replenish/fan-out with a fake provider+store+stubbed fetch (`lib/e2ee/engine.test.ts`, 3), and every new API-client fn (`lib/api/endpoints.test.ts` +11). **Mocked e2e** with a STUBBED crypto layer injected via the test seam (`e2e/e2ee.spec.ts`, 7): availability gating (advertised vs 404), inbox lock badge, device-setup flow → composer, inbound decrypt + per-message **undecryptable** state, disappearing-timer POST body, and the devices settings list+remove. **ONE real-crypto backed spec WRITTEN** (`e2e-backed/e2ee.spec.ts`, write-only, not run in this loop): two browser contexts + real Olm + real backend — A sets up, B sets up, A sends encrypted, B reads plaintext after reload, B's own envelope fetch proves the server holds only ciphertext, a THIRD account's list-messages 404s, and a short-TTL envelope vanishes after its TTL.)

The launched run is POLLED every 2s (`GET /{id}`, one scheduled read per tick, unmount-safe, transient-failure retry) while `pending`/`running`, streaming the **progress spinner** → the **report**: a per-entity counts table (planned/imported/skipped/failed/unsupported, failed>0 flagged red), a conflicts list, and the intentionally-not-migrated (`deferred`) families, plus the detected source-schema-version badge and a **recent-runs** history selector. **Security posture (per the P18 spec + PROMPT.md): the source DB/storage connection lives in SERVER CONFIG only — the browser NEVER asks for, sends, or stores a DSN/credential; the UI only triggers + monitors.** A **503** ("import not configured") renders **operator guidance** pointing at the server config — never a credential form; a **409** ("already running") adopts + monitors the in-progress run. Never display or store source DB credentials in the browser — enforced by the mocked no-password-input assertions + the launch-body unit test. See `../vidra-core/.ralph/specs/peertube-import.md`.)

(Slice `user-federation-1`, 2026-07-03, consuming the core-fed-inbound/core-fed-follow contracts (`/me/remote-follows`, `/remote-videos/{id}[/thumbnail]`, remote-card fields on feed/search/subscriptions — all verified in `../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml`; `check-contract.mjs` green, 95 paths). **Remote cards**: `VideoCard` renders `remote:true` items on every card surface (subscriptions, search, scope=all feed) with the origin-domain badge (globe SVG + `sr-only` "From"), the `name@domain` channel identity as plain text (no local route), the cached poster via `remoteVideoThumbnailUrl`, and links to `/remote/{id}` instead of `/videos/{id}`. **Remote watch surface**: `app/remote/[id]` → `components/RemoteWatchView.tsx` — loads `GET /remote-videos/{id}` (loading/error(retry)/404 "or its origin instance is blocked here" states), plays `stream_url` when present (`lib/remote-playback.ts` pure mode decision — unit-tested ×7 — + `lib/use-remote-playback.ts`: hls.js dynamic import for `.m3u8` over MSE / native HLS fallback / plain `<video src>` for direct files; a fatal origin-stream failure degrades to the link-out panel, no local /original fallback exists), always renders a **"Watch on {domain}"** external link (`target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer"`), and an honest no-stream panel ("This video can't be played here…"). **Honest interactions copy**: "Comments, ratings, and saving live on the origin instance." — deliberately NO comments/rating/save/playlist/report controls on remote videos (v1 per `../vidra-core/.ralph/specs/federation-remote-content.md` §4). `Video` type gained `remote/domain/watch_url/stream_url`; `RemoteVideo` type added. Mocked `e2e/remote-videos.spec.ts` (9: subscriptions remote card badge+link vs local card; search remote card; scope toggle; watch playback+origin link+honest copy; no-stream panel; 404; mute control ×2). TESTED-on-mocks: rendering real ingested remote rows end-to-end awaits a compose-level fake-remote harness (follow-up below); the backend's own httptest federation suite proves the data plane.)

- [x] Implement federated comments display + remote-video moderation surfaces. (Slice `user-federation-2`, 2026-07-03, consuming the core-fed-comments contract — remote comment fields (`Comment.remote/author_domain`, nullable `author_id`) + `POST /remote-videos/{id}/report` + `GET /admin/remote-videos/blocked` + `POST|DELETE /admin/remote-videos/{id}/block`, all verified in `../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml` (`check-contract.mjs` green, 109 paths / 98 referenced). **Federated comments** (`components/CommentsSection.tsx`): a `remote:true` comment renders the author-name snapshot as plain text (no local profile/account link), an origin-domain badge (globe + `sr-only` "From"), and the shared `<ProtocolBadge>`; the account controls (Message/Mute/Block/Report user) are **excluded** — a remote author has no local account (`author_id` null) — and replaced with **Mute instance** (`aria-label` "Mute instance {domain}" → `POST /me/mutes/instances/{domain}`; success hides EVERY comment from that origin locally, matching the backend filter) + **Report** (comment report — the comment row exists locally). A remote author-name snapshot is never treated as the viewer's own (username-collision guard on `isAuthor`). Avatar falls back to the initial letter (no local avatar URL for a null author_id). **Remote-video report**: `ReportButton` gained `kind="remote_video"` (reads as plain "video" to the reporter) and sits on `/remote/[id]` next to Mute instance (`api.reportRemoteVideo`; anon → "Sign in to report"); the honest-copy line now adds "Reports go to the moderators of this instance." — NOTE: this supersedes the v1 "no report control on remote videos" stance from `federation-remote-content.md` §4, sanctioned by the backend shipping the remote_video report contract. **Moderation queue**: `target_type=remote_video` reports render the "remote video" type pill, the origin-domain chip, the title linking to `/remote/{remote_video_id}` (works pre-block; "(remote video unavailable)" when deleted), and a **Block video** action → `POST /admin/remote-videos/{id}/block` carrying the report reason for audit → "Video blocked · Manage" → `/moderation/blocked/remote`. **Blocked remote videos list** (DECISION: a **Remote tab**, not a merged list — row shape/actions differ: a local row links to `/videos/{id}` + channel; a blocked remote row is hidden from `/remote/{id}` so its title links OUT to the origin `watch_url` (`target=_blank rel="noopener noreferrer"`) with the `name@domain` identity + domain shown): `components/BlockedVideosTabs.tsx` (Local | Remote, self-hides for non-mods like ModerationTabs) on `/moderation/blocked` + new `/moderation/blocked/remote` → `components/BlockedRemoteVideosView.tsx` (RoleGate moderator, `GET /admin/remote-videos/blocked`, per-row **Unblock** `aria-label` "Unblock {title}" → `DELETE .../block`, loading/error(retry)/empty states); ModerationTabs keeps "Blocked videos" lit on the sub-tab. API client `reportRemoteVideo`/`getBlockedRemoteVideos`/`blockRemoteVideo`/`unblockRemoteVideo` + `BlockedRemoteVideo(ListResponse)` types + Report gained `remote_video_*` context (unit-tested ×4 new — `endpoints.test.ts`, 117 green). Mocked e2e: `e2e/comments.spec.ts` (+2: remote comment badges + no-profile-link + local row clean; signed-in control swap asserted both ways + instance mute POST hides all comments from that origin, keeps local), `e2e/remote-videos.spec.ts` (+2 & extended: report dialog POSTs `{reason}` to `/remote-videos/rv1/report` + confirmation; anon gets sign-in link; ActivityPub badge on cards/watch), `e2e/moderation.spec.ts` (+1: remote_video report card origin+title+link + Block → `/admin/remote-videos/rv9/block` with `{reason}` body + Manage → blocked/remote), `e2e/blocked-videos.spec.ts` (+3: anon gated no-fetch + tabs hidden; Remote tab lists origin identity + external review link, local list untouched; unblock DELETEs + empty state). Backed: `e2e-backed/blocked-videos.spec.ts` gained a read-only "remote block-list loads (empty) against the real backend" wire-up test (no fake-remote needed); the MUTATING remote flows are fake-remote-gated (below) — instance-mute persistence itself is already backed-proven in `e2e-backed/instance-mutes.spec.ts`.)

Today: UI TESTED-on-mocks; the backend's own httptest federation suite covers the data plane; instance-mute persistence and the remote block-list read are backed-proven without a remote.
- [x] Implement WebFinger-style lookup UI if in-scope. (VERIFIED-as-designed 2026-07-03 (slice user-federation-2 review): the follow-remote input on `/subscriptions` IS the WebFinger-style lookup — `lib/remote-follow.ts` `parseRemoteFollowTarget` (unit-tested ×5) accepts `name@domain`, `@name@domain` (leading @ tolerated — `@user@domain` and `channel@domain` forms are the SAME WebFinger handle shape; the backend resolves either and answers 422 when the target resolves to something that is not a followable channel), `host:port` domains, and actor URLs; obvious non-targets are rejected locally without a round trip ("Enter a name@domain handle or a channel URL."). Resolution errors surface clearly as `role="alert"` copy: 422 → "That channel could not be found. Check the handle or URL and try again.", 503 → "Federation is disabled on this instance.", other errors → the ApiError message. Asserted in `e2e/remote-follows.spec.ts` (parse error, 422, 503 surfaces). No separate lookup page needed — a lookup that can't follow would be a dead end; the follow form is the lookup.)
- [x] Implement ActivityPub visibility labels where needed. (Covered by the `<ProtocolBadge>` pass below: every remote surface is labeled ActivityPub. Remote content is public-only in v1 per `federation-remote-content.md`, so there is no per-item remote visibility variance to label; local videos keep `PrivacyBadge` (public/unlisted/private).)
- [x] Implement ATProto/Bluesky connection settings as Vidra extension. (Slice `user-atproto-ui`, 2026-07-03, consuming the core-atproto v1 contract `PUT|GET|DELETE /api/v1/me/atproto` — `ATProtoLinkRequest`/`ATProtoStatus`, verified in `../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml`; ran `npm run codegen` to refresh `lib/api/generated.ts` for the new schemas. **New page `/settings/connections`** (`app/settings/connections/page.tsx` → `components/ConnectionsView.tsx`, session-gated like the other settings; linked from `SettingsView` via a new **"Connected accounts"** Manage section — distinct from "Connected logins", which is OIDC sign-in providers). A **Bluesky section** labeled with the new `<ProtocolBadge protocol="atproto">`: **connect form** with a `Bluesky handle` field + an `App password` field carrying CLEAR, repeated guidance that it is a Bluesky **APP password, not the main password** (`AppPasswordHelp`, with a "Create an app password" help link to `bsky.app/settings/app-passwords`, `target=_blank rel=noopener`), an **auto-post `Toggle`** (default on; "new PUBLIC videos only"), and an optional Advanced **custom PDS URL** field; on submit → `PUT /me/atproto` (200 → linked status). **Linked status view**: `@handle`, resolved DID, PDS, last-post (`relativeTime` or "No videos announced yet"), connected-time, an always-visible **auto-post toggle** (flipping it reveals an app-password re-auth confirm — the backend re-verifies on every write and never re-exposes the sealed password; documented INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE from a passwordless toggle, forced by the PUT-only contract), a **Reconnect** affordance (reopens the form), and **Unlink** (one-tap-armed → `DELETE /me/atproto`). **Honest errors**: 422 → surfaced with the backend message + an app-password reminder (`mapLinkError`); 502 → "Couldn't reach Bluesky…"; 503 (extension disabled) flips the whole section to an honest not-enabled state (no form). NO posting UI beyond the toggle — cross-posting is automatic on publish. API client `getATProtoAccount`/`linkATProtoAccount`/`unlinkATProtoAccount` + `ATProtoLinkRequest`/`ATProtoStatus` types (unit-tested ×5 — `endpoints.test.ts`, 168 green). Mocked `e2e/connections.spec.ts` (5, all green: link→status shows + app-password guidance + auto-post toggle default-on + PUT body asserted; bad-credential 422 surfaced honestly, stays on form; linked auto-post toggle → re-auth → PUT `auto_post:false`; unlink DELETE → back to form; 503 disabled state, no form). **Backed proof WRITTEN, not run** (`e2e-backed/atproto.spec.ts`): links via the UI against a real vidra-core, proves persistence via a fresh `GET /me/atproto` (DID resolved, `auto_post` set, app password NEVER returned) and that unlink 404s the read — `test.skip()`ped unless `E2E_ATPROTO=true` with `E2E_ATPROTO_HANDLE`/`E2E_ATPROTO_APP_PASSWORD` real throwaway app-password creds (linking hits a LIVE Bluesky PDS — none in CI; never part of `npm run ci`/default `e2e:backed`; documented in-file).)
- [x] Implement clear protocol labeling: ActivityPub, ATProto, both, or local-only. (Slice `user-federation-2`, 2026-07-03: shared `components/ProtocolBadge.tsx` — `protocol: "activitypub" | "local"` pill (share-nodes icon + "ActivityPub" indigo / home icon + "Local only" zinc, `title` long-form explanation) — applied where the ui-inventory expects labels: **remote cards** (`VideoCard` remote branch, next to the origin-domain badge), **remote watch** (`RemoteWatchView`, next to the domain chip), **federated comments** (`CommentsSection` remote rows). ATProto variant SHIPPED (slice `user-atproto-ui`, 2026-07-03): `ProtocolBadge` now covers `"activitypub" | "atproto" | "local"` (ATProto = sky pill + broadcast glyph, "Cross-posted to the Bluesky network via the ATProto protocol"), applied on the `/settings/connections` Bluesky section — completing local-only / ActivityPub / ATProto label coverage. Vidra does not yet INGEST ATProto content, so remote content surfaces (cards/watch/comments) stay ActivityPub; the ATProto label lives where the user opts OUTBOUND into Bluesky. **CONTRACT GAP CLOSED** (slice `user-auth-ui`, 2026-07-03): `InstanceResponse` now carries `federation_enabled` (verified in openapi.yaml) and the instance-level label ships on a new **/about** page (`app/about/page.tsx` → `components/InstanceAboutView.tsx`): `<ProtocolBadge protocol={federation_enabled ? "activitypub" : "local"}>` next to the instance name plus an honest Federation section (federates-over-ActivityPub vs stays-on-this-server copy), instance description/software/registration policy/contact/terms/privacy. Linked from the sidebar bottom ("About", info glyph). Mocked `e2e/about.spec.ts` (4: ActivityPub badge + about fields when federating; "Local only" when not; sidebar entry navigates; instance-fetch failure → retryable error state).)
- [x] Add tests for protocol labels and disabled states. # P12 — Captions, Accessibility, and Internationalization Readiness

- [x] Implement captions management UI. (Studio caption upload/list/remove — `components/CaptionsManager.tsx` on the per-video edit surface (`GET`/`POST`/`DELETE /videos/:id/captions[/:lang]`). **VERIFIED** end-to-end in `e2e-backed/captions.spec.ts`: a creator uploads a WebVTT track from the studio → it persists (visible via the public captions API) → removing it deletes it (captions table confirmed via psql). See P6.1.)
- [x] Implement captions language selector. (`components/CaptionsManager.tsx` now fetches the `/videos/config` taxonomy on mount and renders the caption **language** as a curated `<select>` of the same ISO-639-1 codes the studio metadata dropdowns use — every id (e.g. `en`, `pt`, `zh`) satisfies the backend caption tag validator `^[A-Za-z]{2,3}(-[A-Za-z0-9]{1,8})*$`. Falls back to the free-text input when the config fetch fails/returns no languages. Mocked `e2e/studio.spec.ts` selects from the dropdown; **VERIFIED** against real vidra-core + PostgreSQL in `e2e-backed/captions.spec.ts` (creator picks a language from the `<select>` → caption persists via the public captions API → removal deletes it). The watch-page `<track>` display + native CC toggle already ship (P8.2, `e2e-backed/captions-watch.spec.ts`).)
- [x] Implement Whisper auto-caption request UI if backend supports it. (The Whisper contract landed — `POST`/`GET /videos/:id/captions/auto` + `CaptionJob`. `components/CaptionsManager.tsx` (studio per-video edit surface) gains a **"Generate automatically"** section: a language-hint `<select>` (the curated `/videos/config` taxonomy, "Transcription language"; labelled distinctly from the manual "Caption language" to avoid a selector collision) → `api.requestAutoCaption` (`POST …/captions/auto`, 202). Graceful contract handling: **503** (auto-captioning disabled on the instance) → the control is replaced with "Automatic captions aren't available on this server." and the button is gone (disabled-with-explanation); **409** (a job already running) → it just follows the in-flight job to completion; **422** (bad language tag) → an inline message. API client `requestAutoCaption`/`getAutoCaption` + `CaptionJob`/`CaptionJobResponse`/`AutoCaptionRequest` types added, unit-tested (POST body incl./excl. the hint; GET status URL). Mocked `e2e/studio.spec.ts` (3): request→poll→track appears + "Automatic captions added."; the 503 disabled-with-explanation state; a 409 still polls through to the finished track. Backed spec WRITTEN `e2e-backed/whisper-captions.spec.ts` — `test.skip()`s unless `E2E_WHISPER=true` (documented: needs a `WHISPER_ENABLED` backed stack + a clip with audio) → requests auto-captions from the studio → polls to done → the generated track persists (public captions API). Backend: vidra-core P12 Whisper.)
- [x] Implement caption processing status states. (The auto-caption control drives a small state machine in `CaptionsManager`: idle → **running** ("Generating captions… this can take a few minutes. You'll be notified when they're ready.", polling `GET …/captions/auto` every 2s while the input+button are disabled) → **done** (the list refreshes to reveal the new track + "Automatic captions added.") / **failed** (the job's safe `error` reason) / **unsupported** (503). A real bug caught in review + test: flipping to "done" re-runs the poll effect and aborts its `AbortController`, so the list is refreshed BEFORE the terminal state is set (else the new track never appears until a manual reload). Covered by the mocked auto-caption tests above.)
- [x] Audit keyboard navigation for core routes. (Slice `user-a11y-i18n`, 2026-07-03: swept home/watch/studio/settings/messages/admin. Added a **"skip to content"** link — the first focusable element in the document (`app/layout.tsx`), visually hidden until keyboard focus (`.skip-link` in `globals.css`, off-screen → slides in on `:focus`), targeting a new `#main-content` region (`tabIndex={-1}`) that wraps every page's `<main>`, so keyboard/AT users jump past the header + primary nav. No focus traps or unreachable controls were found: the header mobile menu, Sidebar, and all dialogs/menus already trap/restore correctly (see next item). Mocked `e2e/keyboard-nav.spec.ts`: Tab lands on the skip link first → Enter moves focus to `#main-content`. The skip-link label is routed through `t("a11y.skipToContent")`.)
- [x] Audit focus management for dialogs/dropdowns. (Slice `user-a11y-i18n`: the `Modal` (focus-trap + focus restore + Escape/backdrop) and `Dropdown`/`Tabs`/`PlayerMenu`/`KeyboardShortcutsHelp` (roving focus, Arrow/Home/End, Escape returns focus to the trigger) already handle focus correctly and are RTL-tested. Audit **fixed the stragglers**: the three hand-rolled watch-page dialogs — `ShareButton`, `DownloadButton`, `DonateButton` — focused an internal element and closed on Escape/backdrop but did NOT trap Tab or restore focus to the trigger on close. Migrated all three onto the `Modal` primitive (accessible name preserved via the `<h2>` title → `aria-labelledby`), so they now trap + restore for free and the ad-hoc dialog scaffolding is deleted. Existing `e2e/share-download.spec.ts` + `e2e/donations.spec.ts` stay green against the migrated dialogs.)
- [x] Add ARIA labels to icon-only buttons. (Slice `user-a11y-i18n`: swept every `<button>`/`<Link>` carrying only an icon across `app/` + `components/` (scripted scan for icon children lacking an accessible name). **Clean by construction** — icon-only controls use the `IconButton` primitive (mandatory `label` → aria-label + title), the video player uses native `<video controls>` (browser-labeled), and `SearchBox`/`NotificationsBell`/nav are labeled; the one scan hit was a false positive (dynamic text). Regression guard: the extended axe suite runs the critical `button-name` rule over home/watch/settings/studio/messages/admin.)
- [x] Add reduced-motion friendly interactions. (Slice `user-a11y-i18n`: `globals.css` is the single global motion utility — a `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` reset that neutralizes every animation/transition duration app-wide (Toggle, Toast, Skeleton/Spinner pulse, tab/hover, the new skip-link slide) and forces `scroll-behavior: auto`. The only JS-driven motion, `scrollIntoView`, is already non-smooth. Components do not branch on it themselves. Proven by `e2e/keyboard-nav.spec.ts`: under emulated `reduce` a probe `animate-spin` element resolves to ~0s duration, and to 1s without the preference.)
- [x] Add basic responsive coverage for mobile/tablet/desktop. (Slice `user-a11y-i18n`: `e2e/responsive.spec.ts` exercises the key surfaces — home, watch, studio, messages — at **phone (390px)** and **tablet (768px)** viewports, asserting the primary heading renders and the page does not scroll horizontally (`scrollWidth ≤ clientWidth`). Signed-in surfaces are seeded via the cookie-mode boot refresh so a direct narrow-viewport navigation lands authenticated. Complements the existing `e2e/mobile-nav.spec.ts` (phone hamburger disclosure). Desktop is the default project viewport already covered by the rest of the suite.)
- [x] Add an internationalization-readiness seam (t()/string catalog). (Slice `user-primitives`, P12: `lib/i18n` — `lib/i18n/en.ts` is the single default-locale catalog (dotted keys + `{placeholder}` interpolation), `t(key, vars)` reads it, and the key type `MessageKey` makes an unknown key a **compile error** so the catalog can't silently drift from call sites. NOT a translation effort — readiness only: proves strings are externalizable, swappable per-locale later behind `activeCatalog` with zero call-site changes. Used by the primitives (Modal/Toast close+dismiss) and the report modal (title/reason/submit/thanks). Unit-tested (`lib/i18n/i18n.test.ts`, 4). Documented in `.ralph/specs/design-system.md`. **Extended in slice `user-a11y-i18n`** beyond the primitives so the readiness claim is real across a representative set: the shared app-wide state primitives `Spinner` (loading label) + `ErrorState` (title/retry) route through `t()` — externalizing every loading/error surface in the app at once — plus the app-shell `not-found`/`error` route pages and the skip-link. New keys under `common.*`/`a11y.*`/`state.*`. Coverage matrix (what's routed vs the long tail that remains) documented in `.ralph/specs/design-system.md` ("i18n coverage").)
- [x] Add accessibility checks to Playwright where feasible. (`e2e/a11y.spec.ts` runs **axe-core** (DEPENDENCY ADDED: `@axe-core/playwright@4.12.1`, MPL-2.0, devDependency only — the standard automated-a11y engine, justified per the P12.6 plan) against five route-mocked key surfaces: home (feed cards rendered), watch (player + caption track + comments — the caption track keeps the axe `video-caption` rule satisfied honestly, matching the real player behavior), login, settings (signed in), admin users (admin, list rendered). The gate fails on **serious/critical** violations only (readable dump of rule id/impact/help/nodes on failure); minor/moderate stay non-blocking. Fixes it flagged (all light-mode `color-contrast`, serious): VideoCard "No preview" placeholder `text-zinc-400` on `bg-zinc-200` → `text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400`; the muted `text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500` caption combo (fails on white, and its dark variant was marginal too) → the established `text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400` in AdminUsersView ("joined …", self-edit note), AdminRegistrationRequestsView (requested-at), CommentsSection ("(edited)") — same combo everywhere it existed, so future scans don't flap. Runs in `npm run ci` (part of the chromium project); full suite green after the fixes. **Extended in slice `user-a11y-i18n`** to also scan **studio** (signed-in, channels + create form) and **messages** (signed-in, inbox rendered) — no serious/critical violations, so the automated-a11y net now spans home/watch/login/settings/studio/messages/admin.)

# P13 — Simple Crypto Donation UI

> Slice `user-donations-ui` (2026-07-03): NON-CUSTODIAL, display+copy-only crypto
> donation addresses over the committed `/api/v1/**/donation-addresses` contract
> (curated set bitcoin/ethereum/litecoin/monero; ownership challenge/verify where
> supported — ethereum EIP-191 only, the rest are unverified-only by design).
> Endpoints `api.{listMy,add,delete,challenge,verify}DonationAddress` +
> `listUser/ChannelDonationAddresses` (Vitest: 7 new endpoint tests). Pure
> per-network validator + metadata in `lib/donation-address.ts` (11 unit tests).
> `npm run ci` green.

- [x] Implement donation wallet display on bio/channel pages. (`components/DonateButton.tsx` — a reusable public "Donate" affordance mounted on the channel page (`ChannelView`), fed BOTH the channel-scoped read (`/channels/{handle}/donation-addresses`) and the owner's account-level read (`/users/{id}/donation-addresses`). Renders ONLY when addresses exist; opens an accessible dialog (role=dialog, Escape/click-out close, focus mgmt) listing address(es) per network with copy buttons + verified badge + an honesty line ("never send funds you can't afford to lose / addresses are user-provided; Vidra holds no funds"). No standalone public user-profile route exists yet, so the channel page is Vidra's creator/user identity surface — both public reads are exercised there.)
- [x] Implement add/edit wallet address settings UI. (`/settings/donations` → `components/DonationSettingsView.tsx`: session-gated (restoring/anon/authed), loading/error/empty states; add form (network select + address input + optional label + optional channel-scope select sourced from `getMyChannels`); list of the caller's addresses; per-row delete. Linked from `SettingsView` ("Manage donation addresses"). Edit-in-place is not in the backend contract (no PATCH) — the flow is add/delete, which the contract supports; recorded as an intentional shape.)
- [x] Implement network/address type selector. (Network `<select>` over the curated set (`DONATION_NETWORKS`); per-network client-side format validation (`validateDonationAddress`) surfaces an inline error BEFORE any request, and the backend's 422 message is surfaced on top — backend stays authoritative.)
- [x] Implement verification status badge. (`components/DonationBadge.tsx` — Verified (emerald + check + sr-only "owner") / Unverified (neutral, not alarming); not colour-only. Shown in the settings list AND the public donate dialog.)
- [x] Implement verification challenge UI if backend supports it. (Unverified + verification-supported (ethereum) rows show "Verify ownership" → `POST …/challenge` → the exact message to sign + per-network signing instructions + a signature paste box → `POST …/verify` → badge flips. Honest 501/409/422 handling (expired → request again; wrong sig → retry). Unsupported networks (bitcoin/litecoin/monero) show an explicit "verification is not available" state instead of a dead button.)
- [x] Do not implement custody, balances, payouts, premium subscriptions, or payment processing. (Display + copy only — no amount/balance field is read or shown anywhere; honesty line states Vidra holds no funds and processes no payments; P13 prohibition honored.)
- [x] Add component tests for verified/unverified display. (Mocked `e2e/donations.spec.ts` (8, green): anon prompt; add → row + Unverified badge (+ POST body); per-network validation error blocks the request; verify flow flips Unverified→Verified (+ verify body); unsupported-network honest state; delete → empty state; channel Donate dialog lists + copies an address (clipboard read-back) + honesty line + Verified badge; no Donate button when a channel has no addresses. Plus 11 `lib/donation-address.test.ts` unit tests covering the badge-driving verified flag paths + validators.)
- [x] Add backend-backed e2e proving a saved/edited wallet address and its verification state persist to the database and are reflected in the UI after refetch. (WRITTEN `e2e-backed/donations.spec.ts` + fixtures helpers `{meId,createChannel,myDonationAddresses,channel/userDonationAddresses}`: signup → seed channel → add account-level ETH + channel-scoped BTC via the UI → API read-back proves BOTH rows persisted (scope + verified=false) → public channel/user reads surface them → channel-page Donate dialog lists the address → delete ETH via UI → API read-back proves only BTC remains → hard reload shows the survivor from the server. Runs with the `backend-backed` project / `frontend-e2e-backed.yml`; NOT executed in this loop — per the Critical Verification Rule this stays evidence-pending-a-backed-run, not VERIFIED-on-mocks.)
