# W0 — Design/Template Parity (Backport Programme)

Spec: `.ralph/specs/backport-w0-design-parity.md` — read it (and LOOK at the two
template JPEGs in `.ralph/specs/design/`) before touching any of these. The token
migration is already complete; W0 is visual/layout parity with the templates. Every
task follows the spec's working rules: before/after screenshots (light+dark ×
mobile+desktop) into the git-ignored `.ralph/design-review/w0/<area>/`, tokens only,
e2e + a11y suites updated in the same slice and green, one area per slice.

- [x] W0.1 Set up the parity harness. **DONE 2026-07-07.** `scripts/design-shots.mjs` (npm script `design:shots`) drives a headless chromium over an extensible **area registry** and captures every viewport×theme combination — `mobile` (390×844) + `desktop` (1440×900) × `light` + `dark` — into `.ralph/design-review/w0/<area>/<viewport>-<theme>.png` (added to `.gitignore`). Theme is emulated via Playwright `colorScheme` (the app follows `prefers-color-scheme` when no theme is pinned — `lib/theme.ts` default "system", no `data-theme`), so every `light-dark()` token flips with zero localStorage hacks. Each area installs its own `page.route` mocks (same technique as the mocked e2e specs); an abort fallback on `/api/v1/*` keeps unmocked calls from hanging on a refused backend. It is a dev tool, NOT in `npm run ci`: it lives under `scripts/` (so `no-console` is off and tsc's `.ts` globs skip the `.mjs`), and nothing imports it. **Evidence:** ran against `E2E_PORT=3181 npm run dev` and captured the flagship `home` area — 4 PNGs verified (light/dark × mobile/desktop), correctly themed and sized; they cleanly expose the actual W0.2–W0.4 gaps (no FOLLOWING section, boxed bordered cards, no Live-now rail, missing card avatars). Canonical gate green: typecheck ✓ lint ✓ unit ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e ✓ (3 admin-overview specs flaked under the 365-spec parallel build run but pass 4/4 in isolation — unrelated to this inert change). Add an area by extending `AREAS` in the script.
- [x] W0.2 Desktop app shell to template: sidebar order Home/Trending/Subscriptions/Library/History/Messages/Studio + FOLLOWING section (channel avatars, names, unread/live dot); header with centered pill search ("Search videos, channels, tags"), `+ Create` pill, bell with dot, avatar menu. **DONE 2026-07-07.** The shell production code (sidebar reorder in `components/nav-links.ts`; new `components/SidebarFollowing.tsx` wired to the REAL `GET /me/subscriptions` via `api.listFollowedChannels` + `lib/api` type/client additions; Playlists moved off the sidebar to a Library-page link) landed in prior commit `4396ab4`; this slice verified it against `specs/design/desktop-template.jpeg`, closed the canonical gate, and captured evidence. **Sidebar** — order Home/Trending/Subscriptions/Library/History/Messages/Studio, then a `FOLLOWING` group (28→24px avatar + name, linking to `/channels/[handle]`); renders nothing when signed-out/empty (never adds an empty landmark). **Header** — brand left, centered pill `SearchBox` ("Search videos, channels, tags"), outlined `+ Create` pill, `NotificationsBell` (unread-count dot), `AccountMenu`; already template-conformant, no change needed. **Evidence:** added an authed `shell` area to `scripts/design-shots.mjs` (route-mocks `/auth/refresh`+`/auth/me`+`/me/subscriptions`+`unread-count`) and captured `.ralph/design-review/w0/shell/{mobile,desktop}-{light,dark}.png` + refreshed `home/*`; read the desktop shell PNGs (light+dark) side-by-side with the template JPEG — sidebar order, FOLLOWING group, and header chrome match. **Deviations / dependencies:** (1) the FOLLOWING per-channel unread/live **dot is deliberately not rendered** — the public contract carries no such signal (`Channel`/`FollowedChannel` have no `is_live`/unread field; `getLiveStreams` is owner-only), so per working-rule #4 it is a tracked backend dependency, not stubbed. (2) `AccountMenu` stays the design-system-sanctioned avatar+username+Sign-out affordance rather than the template's click-to-open avatar dropdown: converting it has a ~48-spec suite-wide blast radius (the shared `signIn` helpers assert a visible "Sign out"), which exceeds this vertical slice — deferred as a dedicated follow-up. **Gate-unblock (prior-commit regression, NOT W0.2):** the mocked e2e suite was RED before this slice due to the ungated video-card rework in commit `623a759` — proven independent of W0.2 by an A/B/C scratch experiment (mocking `/me/subscriptions` changed nothing; only clicking the nested card link navigated). Two fixes, committed separately: (a) `components/VideoCard.tsx` — the title heading was wider than its inline link so a center-click/tap landed on empty space (broke ~13 signed-in watch-flow specs and hurt real click targets); moved `line-clamp-2` onto the title `Link` so the whole two-line title is the anchor. (b) `e2e/remote-videos.spec.ts` — the card exposes two same-named links (thumbnail `aria-label` + title), so the watch-link assertions were disambiguated with `.first()`. **Gate:** `npm run ci` ran to completion — typecheck ✓ lint ✓ unit 413/413 ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e 366/367; the sole failure was `admin-overview` (the documented load-flake: 5s expect timeout + full-parallel, no `CI` retries locally), which passes **4/4 in isolation** with everything else green. Token grep guard clean (no raw palette/hex/`dark:` introduced).
- [x] W0.3 Mobile app shell to template: bottom tab bar Home/Search/Create/Inbox/Library as primary navigation (hamburger only for secondary/overflow); large page-title header with bell + avatar. **DONE 2026-07-07.** Audited the existing mobile shell against `specs/design/app-template.jpeg` and closed the one real gap. **Bottom tab bar** (`components/BottomTabBar.tsx`) already matched the template exactly — tab set/order Home/Search/Create/Inbox/Library, the correct glyphs (house / magnifier / rounded-square-plus Create / bell Inbox / filmstrip-play Library), active `aria-current` + `text-fg font-semibold` state, the Inbox unread dot, and `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` padding — so it needed no change; there is no hamburger anywhere (primary nav is the bottom tabs). **Header** (`components/Header.tsx`) was the gap: on phones the "Vidra" wordmark rendered at the smaller desktop size (`text-xl tracking-[-0.045em]`), but the app template shows it as the **large page title** of a compact top row. Bumped the mobile header to a large-title treatment (`text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight`, row `h-16`) while keeping the *exact* desktop wordmark/height behind `sm:` (`sm:text-xl sm:tracking-[-0.045em] sm:h-14`) so the W0.2-verified desktop shell is untouched; the bell + avatar already sat in that top row (compact, right-aligned). "Vidra" stays a `<Link>` to home (not a heading), so page landmarks/headings are unaffected. The mobile `NotificationsBell` popover's `top-16` fixed anchor now sits flush with the taller header. **e2e** (same slice): added a phone-viewport contract lock to `e2e/mobile-nav.spec.ts` asserting the large-title "Vidra" header brand is visible on phones alongside the bottom tabs (role/visibility, not a brittle CSS-class assertion). **Evidence:** captured BEFORE/AFTER via `npm run design:shots -- shell home` against `PORT=3181 npm run dev` (before/ subdirs under `.ralph/design-review/w0/{shell,home}/`, git-ignored); read the AFTER shell PNGs (mobile light+dark) side-by-side with `app-template.jpeg` — the large "Vidra" title + bell + avatar top row and the five-tab bottom bar match; the desktop AFTER PNG confirms the desktop header/sidebar/grid are unchanged. **Gate:** `npm run ci` ran to completion — typecheck ✓ lint ✓ unit ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e 366/368; the two failures were `feed-filters.spec.ts` (`?tag=` chip + sort — desktop-viewport feed tests, orthogonal to this shell change) which pass **5/5 in isolation** (`npx playwright test e2e/feed-filters.spec.ts`, 8.2s), the documented `.poll`-timing flake under the full parallel build run. Token grep guard clean on the changed files (no raw palette/hex/`dark:` introduced). **Deviations / dependencies:** (1) the header bell keeps its numeric **unread-count** badge rather than the template mockup's plain red dot — the count carries more information than a dot, `design-system.md` sanctions an "unread badge", and it is the shared `NotificationsBell` already accepted for the W0.2 desktop shell; regressing it to a dot would touch desktop for less information, so it is a deliberate deviation, not restyled here. (2) The mobile home screen still stacks a `text-2xl` page `<h1>` ("Recent videos") plus the Local/All scope toggle and Category/Language selects below the header — the template's home has no such heading and a single Recent/Popular/Trending chip row; that is the **feed page's** concern and is explicitly W0.4 (feed to template), not shell chrome, so it is left for that slice.
- [x] W0.4 Home/feed to template: pill filter chips (Recent/Popular/Trending, filled=active); "Live now" rail with `● LIVE` badge + "N watching" chip + stream count; borderless rounded-2xl video cards (duration chip bottom-right, IPFS badge where applicable, avatar + title-first two-line metadata); 3-column desktop grid. **DONE 2026-07-07.** Reshaped the home + `/trending` feed surfaces to the template's chip-led layout. **What changed:** (1) `app/page.tsx` + `app/trending/page.tsx` — the visible `text-2xl` page heading (Recent/Popular/Trending videos) is gone; the template leads with the pill chips and shows no title. The `<h1>` is kept but `sr-only` (page still has a programmatic heading for a11y/landmarks/e2e), and the **Recent/Popular/Trending sort chips now LEAD** the surface (their own row, left-aligned) exactly as the desktop+app templates. The federation-scope toggle + category/language filters (both real, tested features the template mockup omits) are demoted to a quieter secondary row *below* the chips. (2) `components/FeedScopeToggle.tsx` — restyled from an accent-fill pill group to the design-system **segmented switcher** (`bg-surface-muted` track, active segment `bg-surface shadow-sm`), so it reads as a quiet binary switcher and no longer competes with the sort chips as a second "filled" selection; DOM contract (role="group" "Feed scope", buttons Local/All with `aria-pressed`) unchanged so `remote-videos.spec` stays green. The `VideoCard` was already template-faithful from the W0.2 rework (borderless `rounded-2xl` thumbnail on the page background, duration chip bottom-right, 36px avatar + title-first two-line `channel · views · age` metadata, 3-col `lg:grid-cols-3` grid) — no change needed. **e2e (same slice):** the sort heading is now `sr-only`, so the 9 heading assertions in `home`/`trending`/`keyboard-nav`/`responsive`/`not-found` specs were moved from `toBeVisible()` → `toBeAttached()` (still asserts the correct per-sort heading text in the a11y tree; the visible active-sort state is carried by the chip's `aria-pressed`, already asserted). Added a `trending` area to `scripts/design-shots.mjs`. **Evidence:** captured BEFORE (`.ralph/design-review/w0/home/before/`) and AFTER (`home/*`, `trending/*`) via `npm run design:shots` against `PORT=3181 npm run dev` — light+dark × mobile+desktop, git-ignored. Read the AFTER PNGs side-by-side with `specs/design/{desktop,app}-template.jpeg`: chips lead top-left, scope toggle is a quiet track, borderless cards on canvas, 3-col grid, generous whitespace — matches; dark theme correctly inverts the active chip (accent flips light-on-dark via tokens). **Gate:** `npm run ci` ran to completion — typecheck ✓ lint ✓ unit 413/413 ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e 364/368. The 4 failures were all in `e2e/studio.spec.ts` (upload/publish/tags flows at :251/:292/:337/:379) — **entirely outside this slice's diff** (git confirms no studio/upload/auth/shell files touched) and they **pass 4/4 in isolation** (`npx playwright test e2e/studio.spec.ts:251 :292 :337 :379`, 16.7s, fresh server) — the documented local `next start` upload-flow load-flake under full parallelism, not a regression. Every spec this slice touches (home, trending, keyboard-nav, responsive, not-found, feed-filters, remote-videos, a11y) passed in the full run. Token grep guard clean on changed files (no raw palette/hex/`dark:` introduced). **Deviations / dependencies:** (1) **No "Live now" rail** was built — there is no public live-stream listing contract (`getLiveStreams` is channel-owner-scoped; `Video`/feed carries no `is_live`, "N watching", or stream-count field), so per working-rule #4 the rail is a tracked **backend dependency**, not stubbed with fake streams. (2) **No `LIVE` / `IPFS` thumbnail overlay badges** on cards — the feed `Video` contract has no live-state or storage/IPFS field; federated remote cards keep their existing origin-domain + ActivityPub `ProtocolBadge` treatment (accepted in W0.2). Both are tracked backend dependencies. **UPDATE (W1.0, 2026-07-07): the IPFS badge is now WIRED** — vidra-core P19 shipped `ipfs_pinned` on the feed/card `Video`, so `VideoCard` renders the top-left media-overlay IPFS pill only when true (see the W1 section's W1.0 note); the LIVE badge stays a tracked backend dependency (no card live-state field yet). (3) The **scope toggle + category/language filters are retained** (demoted) though the template mockup shows neither — they are real, e2e-tested federation/taxonomy features; deleting them would be a functional regression, so they were subordinated rather than removed. (4) The page heading is deliberately `sr-only` (template shows no feed title) rather than deleted, to preserve the a11y page-heading contract.

- [x] W0.12 Auth screens (login/signup/reset/MFA) to template language. **DONE 2026-07-07.** Brought the auth surfaces into line with the App template's standalone auth screens (`.ralph/design-templates/Vidra App.dc.html`, the AUTH block — a design-system source of truth) and the design-system page-title scale. **What changed:** (1) **Login → template lockup + MFA fix** (`app/login/page.tsx` + `components/auth/LoginForm.tsx`): the page is now a thin `<main>` wrapper and the **title lives inside `LoginForm`**, so each auth state owns its heading. The credentials state leads with a centered lockup — `<h1>Sign in</h1>` (design-system `text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight`) + the template tagline **"The quiet home for independent video"** (`text-sm text-fg-muted`) — mirroring the template's login lockup. The **two-factor challenge** state now renders its OWN `<h1>Two-factor authentication</h1>` (bumped from a small `text-base` `<h2>` to the page-title scale) beneath the lock chip, and the previously-competing page-level **"Sign in" h1 no longer sits above it** — the BEFORE shot showed a giant "Sign in" dwarfing the 2FA block; the AFTER is the template's standalone lock + title + subtitle + code + Verify screen. (2) **Signup title scale** (`app/signup/page.tsx`): the off-scale `text-3xl tracking-[-0.05em]` "Create your account" → design-system `text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight`, consistent with every other auth title (reset / reset-confirm / verify were already on this scale). (3) **Reset-confirm de-fork** (`components/auth/ResetPasswordConfirmForm.tsx`): the hand-rolled raw `<label>`+`<input>` pair (a local `inputClass` fork of the field styling) was replaced with the shared **`Input` primitive** — same labels ("New password" / "Confirm new password"), same rounded-xl token styling, with the mismatch error now anchored to the confirm field via the primitive's `error` prop (removes a forked pattern per the design-system "do not fork these primitives" rule). **e2e (same slice):** no spec edits were required — every auth assertion is by role/label/text, all of which are preserved: `auth.spec.ts` (button "Sign in"/"Create account"/"Reset password", labels, "Reset your password" heading), `mfa-login.spec.ts` (heading "Two-factor authentication" — `getByRole("heading")` matches the promoted h1), `oauth.spec.ts` (provider buttons, landings), and `a11y.spec.ts` (login axe: the Sign-in button gate + no severe violations; the moved h1 + tagline stay axe-clean). Extended the screenshot harness with an `act` hook + six auth areas (`auth-login`, `auth-mfa` [drives the login form into its challenge state], `auth-signup`, `auth-reset`, `auth-reset-confirm`, `auth-verify`) in `scripts/design-shots.mjs`. **Evidence:** captured BEFORE (`.ralph/design-review/w0/auth-*/before/`) and AFTER (`auth-*/*`), light+dark × mobile+desktop (git-ignored), against `PORT=3181 npm run dev`. Read the AFTER PNGs side-by-side with the App template AUTH block: login = centered "Sign in" + tagline lockup over the credential fields + OAuth "or"-divider block; MFA = standalone lock chip + title + subtitle + code + Verify (no competing "Sign in"); signup = restrained `text-2xl` title over the field stack + approval note + OAuth; reset-confirm = the two shared `Input` fields + pill Reset button. Dark theme inverts via tokens (accent flips light-on-dark), no horizontal overflow at 390. **Gate:** `npm run ci` ran to completion — typecheck ✓ lint ✓ unit **413/413** ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e **368/368** on the first full run; a re-run flaked **367/368** on the sole `quarantine.spec.ts:255` ("video_rejected notification" — a `NotificationsBell` popover timeout, **outside this slice's diff**, W0.10 territory) which passes **6/6 in isolation** (`npx playwright test e2e/quarantine.spec.ts`, 14.0s), the documented full-parallel popover flake. The 40 specs this slice actually touches — `auth` + `mfa-login` + `oauth` + `a11y` — pass **40/40 in isolation** (26.3s). Token grep guard clean on the changed files (no raw palette/hex/`dark:` introduced). **Deviations / dependencies:** (1) **login keeps the "Sign in" heading + tagline rather than the template's giant "Vidra" brand lockup** — the auth pages render inside the persistent app shell (the `Header` already carries the Vidra wordmark, large-title on mobile), so a second hero "Vidra" would duplicate it; the tagline + centered treatment adopt the template's voice without the redundant brand. (2) **auth buttons stay `rounded-full` pills** (the `Button`/OAuth primitives) rather than the template's `rounded-xl` (12px) auth buttons — the design-system is the component/shape authority (`rounded-full` for buttons) and forking the primitive is disallowed; a deliberate, documented shape deviation. (3) **signup's title stays page-owned** (only restyled) while login's moved into the form — login had a real in-flow competing-title defect (MFA) that forced the move; signup's default form already shows a single clean title, so the minimal change was a scale fix. (4) **"Forgot your password?" stays under the password field** (not moved to a centered position after the button as in the template) — the current placement is a standard, accessible pattern and moving it is churn with marginal benefit.

- [x] W1.1 [PLAY-02 surface — custom player shell; prerequisite for W1.2–W1.8] **DONE 2026-07-07.** Built `components/player/` and replaced the native-controls `<video>` in `WatchView` AND `EmbedPlayer` (embed = `variant="embed"`, same custom chrome sized to fill the iframe, minus the watch-only theater which lands in W1.U3). **New files:** `components/player/VideoPlayer.tsx` (the shell), `SeekBar.tsx`, `VolumeControl.tsx`, `OverlayButton.tsx` (white-on-scrim icon control), `lib/player-ui.ts` (pure seek/volume/buffered geometry helpers). **Shell:** a chrome-less `<video>` (no `controls`, no `crossOrigin` — the blob-URL `<track>` caption technique is preserved) in a `relative aspect-video rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-black` container (embed: `h-full`); a bottom control bar over a `from-black/80` gradient scrim with **auto-hide** — pinned while paused, while focus is inside the bar (covers any open menu, whose button/items hold focus), and for ~3s after pointer activity; the countdown starts on the `play` event and is cleared on `pause`; hide is **opacity-only** (never `display`), so focus is never lost, and the global reduced-motion reset neutralizes the fade (no manual branch). **Controls:** play/pause (bar button + a click on the video surface — no double-fire, since the video's own `onClick` toggles and the bar is a sibling above it) with a decorative center play affordance while paused; a **seek bar** (`SeekBar`) painting buffered ranges (from `video.buffered`) as a lighter white band under the white played fill + playhead, pointer scrub with pointer-capture, a hover/scrub/keyboard-focus timestamp tooltip, and full keyboard operability (`role="slider"` `tabIndex=0` `aria-label="Seek"` `aria-valuemin/max/now` + `aria-valuetext="1:23 of 12:40"`, ArrowLeft/Right ±5s, Home/End); **volume** (mute `OverlayButton` with `aria-pressed` + a `role="slider"` level slider, ±5% ArrowUp/Down + Home/End, session-local, slider hidden `< sm` so the phone bar stays uncrowded); a `tabular-nums` **time readout** `current / duration`; a **captions toggle** (`aria-pressed`, reusing the existing blob-URL `<track>` loading — its state is synced from the element's `textTracks` `change` event so the button and the `C` shortcut stay in lockstep); a **settings cluster** (Speed + Quality via `PlayerMenu`'s new `variant="overlay"` — real menu semantics: focus moves in, arrows navigate, Escape closes and restores focus to the button; the menu panel is a token `bg-surface-raised` popover, height-capped + scrollable so it never overflows a short player); and **fullscreen on the CONTAINER** (so the custom chrome survives fullscreen — `fullscreenchange` drives the toggle's `aria-pressed`/icon; the `F` shortcut targets the container too). **HLS QUALITY SELECTOR (centerpiece):** adapted `lib/use-hls-playback.ts` from the hard `hls.currentLevel` switch to the smooth `hls.nextLevel` + `LEVEL_SWITCHED` **pending model** — recorded as a deliberate behaviour change (smooth switch at the next fragment instead of a buffer flush). The hook now exposes `activeHeight` + `pending`; `QualityMenu` renders Auto + one entry per rung from `buildLevelMenu` (tallest first), the label shows a busy "…" until `LEVEL_SWITCHED` confirms a manual pick and `Auto (720p)` once the active ABR rung height is known (pure `qualityLabel()` in `lib/hls.ts`); still hidden in native-HLS/original modes. **Dependency (recorded):** real multi-rendition switching needs the backend ladder to emit >1 rung (vidra-core W1.C0) — so the selector is verified against a **synthetic 3-rung master** (`e2e/hls.spec.ts` extended to 1080p/720p/480p) and the backed `hls-playback.spec` asserts whatever rung count the real pipeline produces (flips to ≥2 once that investigation closes); this slice is NOT blocked by it. **Old-player defects BANNED (verified none present):** no `cyan-400`/ad-hoc hues (only the documented media-overlay whites/black-scrims), `role="slider"` sliders are `tabIndex=0` (not −1), menus are focus-managed, every control is a ≥44×44pt hit area (`h-11 w-11` buttons; the sliders use a 44pt-tall transparent hit area around a thin visual line), no `crossOrigin` on the media element, buffered ranges painted, no global `stopPropagation`. **Design guardrail:** the ONLY non-token colors are the documented media-overlay exceptions (white controls/fills on the video, `bg-black` scrims); `.focus-ring` on every control; no raw palette/hex/`dark:` introduced (grep-clean on changed files). `WatchView` keeps the `StoryboardPreview` strip + `KeyboardShortcutsHelp` + Resume below the player for U1 (W1.U4 folds the storyboard into the seek tooltip and removes the strip). **Tests — unit (RTL + pure):** `lib/player-ui.test.ts` (15), `lib/hls.test.ts` `qualityLabel` (5), `components/player/SeekBar.test.tsx` (5 — aria contract, arrow/Home/End seek, clamp, buffered band), `VolumeControl.test.tsx` (4 — mute label/aria, ±5% steps, muted 0%), `VideoPlayer.test.tsx` (6 — chrome-less video, quality/captions gating, play/pause state machine, speed apply + relabel, and the paused-pin/auto-hide timer). **Tests — mocked e2e:** rewrote `e2e/hls.spec.ts` (3-rung selector; embed now runs the shell WITH a quality menu — the old "no menu" assertion is gone); added `e2e/player-shell.spec.ts` (custom Play drives playback, Mute toggles `video.muted`, Captions toggles the track `mode` + `aria-pressed`, the seek bar keyboard-drives `currentTime`, **fullscreen targets the CONTAINER** via a `data-testid` probe, and Tab order Seek→Play→Mute→Volume with visible focus); existing `watch-player.spec` (speed/keyboard/captions/storyboard/related) and `watch`/`embed`/`responsive`/`share-download` all pass unchanged against the shell; **axe** (`a11y.spec` watch) green — serious/critical zero on `/videos/[id]` with the new controls. Backed e2e (`hls-playback`/`embed`/`captions-watch`, not run here — no live stack) stay compatible by construction: the `Quality: Auto`/`{h}p` names, `video` count, `blob:` src, title link and `<track>` selectors are all preserved. **Evidence:** extended the design-shots harness with an HLS mock on the `watch` area + a new `embed` area; captured `.ralph/design-review/w1/{watch,embed}/` light+dark × mobile+desktop and **READ every PNG** — the bespoke white-on-scrim shell with the **quality selector ("Auto")**, seek bar, time readout and center play, no native chrome/kebab; mobile fits (volume slider hidden `< sm`; tightened the time readout + menu padding so the fullscreen icon is no longer edge-clipped at 390px). - [x] W1.2 [PLAY-03] Playback speed 0.25×–4×: **DONE 2026-07-07.** Extracted the rate ladder into a single shared pure module `lib/player-rates.ts` — `PLAYBACK_RATES = [0.25,0.5,0.75,1,1.25,1.5,1.75,2,2.5,3,3.5,4]` (the source W1.6's settings Select + core W1.C3 validation must stay in lockstep with), `rateLabel` (now `1×`, not the old "Normal" — per the spec's button example), `isPlaybackRate`, `stepPlaybackRate(current,direction)` (the `<`/`>` ladder-step **helper landing here, keys wired in W1.8**: next rung strictly greater/less than `current`, snapping off-ladder inputs and clamping at both ends), and the session-persistence trio. **SpeedMenu** now imports the ladder + label from the shared module (removing its local copies) and the button reads `Speed: 1×`/`Speed: 1.5×`; it lives in the shell's overlay settings cluster (`variant="overlay"`) unchanged. **Apply path unchanged & correct:** the shell's `[speed, playback.src]` effect sets `el.defaultPlaybackRate = el.playbackRate = speed`, so the rate is re-applied on every src change — which is exactly how it survives an HLS→original fallback. **Persistence (session scope — recorded stop-gap):** the chosen rate is remembered for the browsing session via `sessionStorage["vidra.player.speed"]`, read through `useSyncExternalStore` (the house pattern — mirrors Sidebar's collapse pref: `serverRate` SSR snapshot = default so NO hydration mismatch, `readStoredRate` the client snapshot, `storeRate` writes + broadcasts a `vidra:player-speed` event so every player in the tab re-reads; a corrupt/off-ladder stored value falls back to 1, the same guard W1.6 will apply to the server value). **DEPENDENCY (recorded):** W1.6's per-user `default_speed` (GET/PUT /me/player-settings, backed by core W1.C3) supersedes this session stop-gap. The spec's own W1.U2 text only required src-change survival; session persistence added per the orchestrator directive. **Tests — unit:** new `lib/player-rates.test.ts` (10 — ladder shape, `isPlaybackRate`, `rateLabel` incl. `1×`, `stepPlaybackRate` up/down/clamp/off-ladder-snap, session round-trip + corrupt/off-ladder fallback); `components/player/VideoPlayer.test.tsx` extended (full 12-rung ladder asserted in menu order, select `4×` ⇒ `playbackRate===4` + `defaultPlaybackRate===4` + persisted `"4"`, and restore-from-session on mount ⇒ `Speed: 2×` + `playbackRate===2`). **Tests — mocked e2e:** `e2e/watch-player.spec.ts` speed test rewritten (button reads `Speed: 1×`, menu offers **12** rungs with `1×` checked, select `4×` ⇒ button `Speed: 4×` + `video.playbackRate===4` + `sessionStorage` shows `"4"`); new `e2e/hls.spec.ts` test "the chosen playback speed survives an HLS→original fallback" (detail advertises `hls_url`, master resolves to a delayed 404, pick `4×` while hls.js is in flight, assert the element degrades to `…/original` AND `playbackRate===4` on the newly-loaded stream). **Gate:** `CI=1 npm run ci` on the final tree — typecheck ✓ lint ✓ unit ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e **385 passed + 1 flaky (retry-absorbed), 0 failed, exit 0** ✓. The lone flaky (`studio.spec.ts:872` HLS-renditions edit surface — UNRELATED to this slice) passes in isolation (47s) — flake rule, noted honestly. **Evidence:** added a `watch-speed` design-shots area (reuses the watch mock; `act` opens the Speed menu) and captured `.ralph/design-review/w1/watch-speed/{mobile,desktop}-{light,dark}.png` — **READ all key PNGs**: the overlay Speed button reads **`1×`** (gauge icon), the token-surfaced menu (light + dark) lists the extended ladder auto-scrolled to the checked `1×` with `1.25×/1.5×/1.75×/2×…` below the height-capped scroll fold (rungs up to `4×` reachable), 44pt targets, quiet-luxury HIG, no raw colors outside the media-overlay exception. - [x] W1.3 [PLAY-04 + PLAY-05] Theater mode + Picture-in-Picture on the shell: **DONE 2026-07-07.** **Theater store (`lib/player-theater.ts`, new):** a session-only external store mirroring `lib/player-rates` — `subscribeTheater`/`serverTheater`/`readStoredTheater`/`setTheater`/`toggleTheater` over `sessionStorage["vidra.theater"]` (`"1"`/`"0"`; a corrupt/unexpected value reads as off — the same guard the speed store uses), broadcasting a `vidra:theater` event so the shell button and the page layout re-read in lockstep with **no context provider / prop-drilling**. `serverTheater` is always `false`, so the SSR snapshot matches the pre-hydration client (no mismatch) and the client restores the session mode after hydration. **WatchView:** reads the store via `useSyncExternalStore`; the outer container gains `data-theater="on|off"` and, in theater, drops `lg:flex-row` → a single full-width column so the stage widens to the full content width and the related rail reflows below. **RelatedVideos:** a new `belowLayout` prop drops the fixed `lg:w-[344px]` rail cap and switches the card `<ul>` to a `lg:grid-cols-3` grid when the rail reflows below (a full-width row of cards instead of the narrow right rail). The card renders at `z-30` over the controls; the decorative centre-play glyph is suppressed while it shows; on dismiss/replay focus returns to the first control (`replayVideo` seeks 0 + plays, `dismissEndCard` just closes). **Countdown (`EndCard`):** auto-advances only while `nextVideo && autoplayEnabled && !cancelled`; the tick is **skipped while `document.hidden`**, so a backgrounded tab is never yanked to a new video (the spec's sanctioned hidden-tab behaviour) and `remaining` can only hit 0 during a visible tick, where a nav effect fires `router.push(nextVideoHref)` exactly once (a remote "next" routes to `/remote/{id}`, local to `/videos/{id}`). Play now navigates immediately; Cancel (or the first Escape) is a hard stop that keeps the card with Replay; a second Escape dismisses. The countdown resets to 8 via the React-endorsed adjust-state-during-render pattern (no reset effect). **A11y:** focus moves to Play now (or Replay) on show; the countdown is announced through a polite `role="status"` live region ("Playing next in 8 seconds", singular at 1, `sr-only` so the visible number is not double-read); Escape cancels then dismisses; the "Autoplay next" control is a `role="switch"` with `aria-checked` + a stable `aria-label`; every button is an `h-11` (≥44pt) target with `.focus-ring`. **Autoplay setting (interim scope):** `lib/player-autoplay.ts` mirrors `lib/player-theater` — a per-session store with a **baked default of ON** (`serverAutoplay`/unset both read true, so the SSR/first-client snapshot matches; only the explicit "0" marker turns it off), consumed via `useSyncExternalStore`; the on-card switch writes it. **DEPENDENCY (recorded):** W1.U6's signed-in `autoplay_next` (GET/PUT /me/player-settings, backed by vidra-core W1.C3 — ABSENT from openapi as of 2026-07-07) supersedes this session stop-gap once it lands. **Design guardrail:** the card lives entirely in the documented media-overlay exception zone (white text/controls on a `bg-black/80` scrim) — no theme tokens (it sits on the video, not a surface), no raw palette/hex/`dark:` introduced (grep-clean on changed files), theme-invariant. **Tests — unit:** `lib/end-card.test.ts` (6 — next-selection incl. empty/null, local vs remote href, countdown pluralise/clamp/round), `lib/player-autoplay.test.ts` (5 — ON default, persist round-trip, toggle, corrupt-stays-ON, broadcast/unsubscribe), `components/player/EndCard.test.tsx` (8 — countdown→navigate, Play-now immediate, Cancel halts + Replay + no nav over 20s, autoplay-off suppresses countdown but Play-now still navigates, **hidden-tab pauses + never navigates**, switch toggles + aria-checked, Escape cancel-then-dismiss, replay-only no-next), `components/player/VideoPlayer.test.tsx` +2 (end card appears on `ended` with the next video + cleared on `play`; replay-only card on no-next + cleared on `loadstart`). - [x] W1.6 [PLAY-07 UI — UNBLOCKED: vidra-core W1.C3 landed (`GET/PUT /api/v1/me/player-settings` present in `vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml`, read fresh at execution time)] **Player settings UI + session-store migration: DONE 2026-07-08.** **Contract (fresh-read):** `GET /me/player-settings` → `PlayerSettings {autoplay_next, default_speed, default_quality, captions_default, theater_default}` (always 200 with the full object; a never-saved user gets the built-in defaults — no 404); `PUT` is a MERGE (`UpdatePlayerSettingsRequest`, every field optional — an omitted field keeps its stored value, a supplied one replaces it, the first PUT creates the row), returning the full effective object; `400` on an off-ladder `default_speed` or a `default_quality` that isn't `"auto"`/`^[0-9]{2,4}p$`. **API layer:** `generated.ts` already in sync (re-ran `node scripts/codegen.mjs` → byte-for-byte, no diff); exported `PlayerSettings` + `UpdatePlayerSettingsRequest` in `lib/api/types.ts`; added `api.getPlayerSettings(signal?)` + `api.updatePlayerSettings(patch)` (PUT) in `lib/api/endpoints.ts`. **Core module `lib/player-settings.ts` (new):** `DEFAULT_PLAYER_SETTINGS` (the baked defaults — 1×, autoplay on, Auto, captions off, theater off — identical to the pre-W1.6 player behaviour AND the server's built-in defaults); a low-level effective-settings holder (`getPlayerSettingsSnapshot`/`hydratePlayerSettings`/`resetPlayerSettings`, broadcasting a `PLAYER_SETTINGS_EVENT`) + a `usePlayerSettings()` `useSyncExternalStore` hook (SSR/pre-hydration = baked, so signed-out is unchanged and there's no hydration mismatch); `QUALITY_OPTIONS` (Auto + 2160/1440/1080/720/480/360p for the Select); and the pure `matchQualityLevel(default_quality, levels)` mapping (unknown/unavailable rung ⇒ `AUTO_LEVEL`). Deliberately imports NOTHING from the three session stores (they import the getter — one direction, no cycle). **THE MIGRATION (interim session-store stop-gaps → per-user backend):** `lib/player-{rates,theater,autoplay}` now treat their `sessionStorage` value as an in-session OVERRIDE and fall back to the effective per-user default when it's UNSET — `readStoredRate` (unset/corrupt/off-ladder ⇒ `normalizePlaybackRate(getPlayerSettingsSnapshot().default_speed)`, the new `normalizePlaybackRate` giving the "invalid speed ⇒ 1" guard), `readStoredTheater` (`"1"`/`"0"` win, else `theater_default`), `readStoredAutoplay` (`"1"`/`"0"` win, else `autoplay_next`); each `subscribe*` also re-reads on `PLAYER_SETTINGS_EVENT`. Un-hydrated the effective default IS the baked default, so **every existing store test stays green** — the per-user value only diverges once a signed-in user's settings hydrate; an in-session pick (SpeedMenu, shell theater toggle, end-card switch) still wins for the session. **Shell consumption on mount:** `WatchView` hydrates the signed-in user's settings (authed ⇒ `GET` → `hydratePlayerSettings`; anon/sign-out ⇒ `resetPlayerSettings` so a shared tab never inherits the previous user's prefs; failures silent — the player keeps baked defaults). `VideoPlayer` reads `usePlayerSettings()` and applies **default_quality** (once the HLS levels parse, once per video id via a ref-guard: a rung this video offers ⇒ `setLevel`, else stay Auto — never overriding a later manual pick) and **captions_default** (enable the first `<track>` once per video); speed/theater/autoplay flow through the session-store fallback (so they need no shell changes). **Settings surface:** new `/settings/playback` page + `components/PlayerSettingsView.tsx` — the design's grouped-rows card (surface-muted, hairline dividers): Autoplay next (Toggle), Default speed (Select over the shared `PLAYBACK_RATES` ladder), Default quality (Select over `QUALITY_OPTIONS`, preserving an off-preset stored value), Captions on by default (Toggle), Theater mode by default (Toggle). Each control saves ONLY its field (a merge-PUT — verified in unit + e2e) optimistically, reconciles from the server's full response, reverts + surfaces an error on failure, and `hydratePlayerSettings`es on success so any open player updates live; session-gated (signed-out ⇒ sign-in prompt, no fetch) with loading/error+retry states. A "Playback" nav row added to `SettingsView`'s Account group. **Tests — unit:** `lib/player-settings.test.ts` (9 — holder hydrate/reset + broadcast, `QUALITY_OPTIONS`, `matchQualityLevel` match/auto/**unknown-rung⇒Auto**/empty-levels); `lib/player-rates.test.ts` (+3 — `normalizePlaybackRate` **invalid⇒1**, per-user `default_speed` fallback + in-session override, invalid hydrated default normalized); `lib/player-theater.test.ts` + `lib/player-autoplay.test.ts` (+1 each — per-user fallback + in-session override); `components/PlayerSettingsView.test.tsx` (7 — anon gate/no-fetch, load+populate all 5, per-field merge-PUT for a toggle/speed/quality, revert-on-error, initial-load retry); `components/player/VideoPlayer.test.tsx` (+2 — **starts at the user's default_speed** when no session pick, **opens theater by default** from `theater_default`); `lib/api/endpoints.test.ts` (+2 — GET target + PUT method/merge body). Full unit suite **683/683**. **Tests — mocked e2e `e2e/player-settings.spec.ts` (3):** anon prompt; the settings page renders every control at its stored value and each change **merge-PUTs exactly one field** (autoplay/speed-as-number/quality); and **the bespoke player on the watch page starts at the user's `default_speed` (1.5×)** — `Speed: 1.5×` + `video.playbackRate===1.5` — proving the store→shell wiring end to end in a real browser. `e2e/a11y.spec.ts` (+1 — the playback settings page passes axe serious/critical). **Tests — backed e2e (DB-proof) `e2e-backed/player-settings.spec.ts`** (+ a `playerSettings` fixture): sign up → `/settings/playback` → set 1.5× → **PUT → API re-GET shows `default_speed=1.5`** (the `player_settings` row changed, other fields kept by the merge) → **hard reload refetches 1.5** (server state, not optimistic) → seed a transcoded public video (`waitForHls`) and open it signed-in ⇒ `video.playbackRate===1.5` → **toggle back to 1× ⇒ PUT ⇒ API shows 1**. (Runs under `--project=backend-backed` with a live vidra-core; NOT part of `npm run ci`, which is mocked-only — written against the fresh real contract.) **Design guardrail:** tokens only (reused the `Toggle`/`Select` primitives; no raw palette/hex/`dark:` — grep-clean on all changed files), axe green, 44pt targets, `prefers-reduced-motion` respected via the shared primitives. **Deviation (recorded):** the Playback surface is a sub-page (`/settings/playback` + nav row) rather than an inline section on `/settings`, matching the notifications/donations/security precedent (interactive controls behind a grouped nav row). **Evidence:** added a `settings-playback` design-shots area → `.ralph/design-review/w1/settings-playback/` light+dark × mobile+desktop and **READ desktop-light + mobile-dark**: the grouped surface-muted card with hairline dividers, the 46×28 toggles (accent fill + canvas knob, correctly inverting on↔off and light↔dark), the inset white/dark selects with chevrons showing `1.5×` / `1080p`, title + subtitle + footer caption; mobile shows the card full-bleed with the bottom tab bar unobstructed. **Gate:** `E2E_PORT=3181 CI=1 npm run ci` on the final tree — typecheck ✓ lint ✓ lint:icons ✓ unit **683/683** ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e **428 passed, 0 failed, 0 flaky, exit 0** ✓. **Closes the interim-persistence dependency recorded in W1.2/W1.3/W1.5** — the per-user `default_speed`/`theater_default`/`autoplay_next` now supersede the session stop-gaps for signed-in users (session picks still win in-session).

# W2 — Upload & import (Backport Programme)

Spec: `.ralph/specs/backport-w2-upload-import.md` — read it (and the core twin
`vidra-core/.ralph/specs/backport-w2-upload-import.md`) before touching any of
these. Built on the DR9 studio vocabulary (commit `69aeae5`: dashed dropzone,
byte-detail progress line, status pills, storage card) — never from scratch.
Every task: vertical slice; consumes only endpoints already merged in
`vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml`; checkbox flips only with (a) unit tests, (b) a
mocked Playwright e2e, (c) for data-mutating flows a backend-backed e2e
(`e2e-backed/`, `npm run e2e:backed`) proving the DB/server state changed AND the
UI shows it after refetch; design guardrail (tokens only, templates canonical,
no emoji/`dark:`; before/after screenshots light+dark × mobile+desktop into
`.ralph/design-review/w2/<area>/` for changed screens); `npm run codegen` after
any contract change; `npm run ci` green locally AND on branch CI, pushed, before
the box ticks.

Backend status (grep-verified in `vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml` 2026-07-08): the
whole W2.C backend wave has LANDED, so no UI slice below is BLOCKED — each records
the core commit + endpoint it binds to (W1-style):
- W2.C1 yt-dlp platform-URL import — `POST /api/v1/videos/{id}/import` gains
  `resolver` (auto|direct|ytdlp) + `import_job.stage`; stable 503 code when the
  resolver is disabled on the instance (commit `10d403d`).
- W2.C2 server-side draft recovery — `GET /api/v1/me/uploads` + `file_fingerprint`
  on the upload session (commit `af875b1`).
- W2.C3 batch-upload guard — 429 `too_many_active_uploads` on the active-session
  cap (commit `71d950b`).
- W2.C4 channel auto-sync — `/api/v1/channel-syncs` CRUD + `/{id}/sync-now`
  (commit `1a4ebc2`).
- W2.C5 thumbnail frame-pick — JSON `{at_seconds}` variant on
  `POST /api/v1/videos/{id}/thumbnail` (commit `3f3c6e6`).

- [x] W2.U1 [UPLOAD-02/03 UI — UNBLOCKED: vidra-core W2.C2 landed (commit

      (a) **WebCrypto fingerprint:** new `lib/api/fingerprint.ts` —
      `computeFileFingerprint` hashes an 8-byte LE size header + the first & last
      1 MiB (SHA-256 hex, the documented opaque recipe; never reads the whole
      file), plus `findUploadByFingerprint`. `resumable-upload.ts` computes it and
      sends `file_fingerprint` on session create AND caches it in the
      `StoredUploadSession` (localStorage demoted to a cache — the server list is
      now the recovery source of truth). (b) **Endpoint:** `api.listMyUploads(fingerprint?)`
      → `ActiveUploadsResponse` (types re-exported). (c) **Recovery card:**
      `components/UploadRecoveryCard.tsx`, rendered in `StudioView` right below the
      storage card (same surface-muted vocabulary): reads `GET /api/v1/me/uploads`
      on load and lists each unfinished session — filename, a 5px progress bar
      (received-chunk count → bytes) + "X of Y" detail, a 24h expiry countdown, and
      Resume/Discard. Resume re-picks the file (browsers can't reopen a handle
      after reload — the card says so), VERIFIES identity by fingerprint (falls
      back to name+size for pre-fingerprint sessions), then resumes via
      `resumableUpload({resume})` with a live progress bar + Cancel, honestly
      guarding a returned `state=failed` (never "Published!"); on finish it remounts
      MyVideosSection so the video appears. Discard DELETEs the session + best-effort
      deletes the orphaned draft. A load failure / empty list hides the card (never
      a faked recovery surface). (d) **Tests:** unit `lib/api/fingerprint.test.ts`
      (9 — determinism, size/head/tail sensitivity, empty file, matcher);
      `resumable-upload.test.ts` updated (create now sends a 64-hex fingerprint);
      mocked `e2e/upload-draft-recovery.spec.ts` (4 — card lists a server session;
      re-pick resumes sending only the MISSING chunks + "Upload finished." link;

      + drops the row). (e) **Backed DB-proof:** `e2e-backed/upload-draft-recovery.spec.ts`
      seeds a half-finished session via the API (bytes localStorage never knew),
      HARD-RELOADs the studio (cookie refresh) so the card lists the server-side
      session, resumes to completion, and reads the finalised video back from

      `api.importVideoFile(videoId, url, resolver="auto")` now sends

      direct vs platform; the backend probes and falls back to yt-dlp when enabled.
      (c) **Stage rail:** new pure `lib/import-status.ts` (`IMPORT_STAGES`,
      `importActiveStage`, `importResolved`, `isImportsDisabledError`) maps

      → downloading → scanning & processing); `ImportStageRail` renders it with the
      studio's spinner+check+pill vocabulary (active step `aria-current="step"`),
      fed by an `onJob` callback threaded through the poll loop. (d) **Safe error +
      Retry:** a failed job shows the backend's SAFE `error` verbatim (never
      "Published!") with a "Retry import" button that re-enqueues against the SAME
      draft via the same endpoint (`runImport`/`retryImport` share the path; the
      draft survives a failed import). (e) **Metadata prefill:** once resolving
      passes (`importResolved`), a one-shot draft refetch prefills title/description
      — but ONLY empty fields (user edits win). Poster is deliberately not shown:
      per the core twin spec the resolver fetches no poster (Process derives it from
      the downloaded original). (f) **Honest disabled state:** the URL tab reads
      `GET /instance` `features.imports`; when off it renders an `EmptyState`
      "Imports are disabled on this instance" and disables Publish — never a dead
      form; the stable 503 `service_unavailable` at submit is the defensive fallback
      to the same state. The URL field also gains paste-detect (a pasted http(s)
      URL is trimmed). (g) **Tests:** unit `lib/import-status.test.ts` (14 — stage

# P-MSG2 — Messaging v2 (proper messenger)

## Operating notes
- [ ] Read `.ralph/specs/messaging-v2.md` before any P-MSG2 slice; keep `design-system.md` and the encrypted-thread branch (`EncryptedThreadView`) working in every slice.
- [ ] Contract-first: slices marked "needs core" consume `vidra-core` deltas (dimensions, before_id, has_avatar) ONLY once they appear in the regenerated `lib/api/generated.ts`; until then ship the documented degraded behavior — never stub fake contract fields.
- [ ] Canonical gates for every slice: full mocked suite green, `npm run ci` green, pushed; backed specs run via `npm run e2e:backed` where the slice mutates data.

## P-MSG2.1 Timeline model (grouping + separators)
- [x] Add `lib/messaging/grouping.ts` + `time.ts` (pure `buildTimeline`: runs = same sender ≤ 5 min gap; separators on day change or > 60 min gap; NEW divider before first unread) with exhaustive vitest coverage (run boundaries, day/year label matrix, tombstones, empty list). **(W0.10a)**
- [x] Refactor the thread list into `MessageTimeline`/`MessageGroup`/`MessageBubble` rendering runs with position-dependent radii (18px base, 6px inner corners, tail corner on last-of-run only), 2px intra-run / 12px inter-run / 20px separator spacing; REMOVE in-bubble timestamps; add hidden `<time datetime title>` + accessible name "{sender}, {time}: {content}" per message. **(W0.10a)**
- [x] Update mocked `e2e/messaging.spec.ts` fixtures/selectors in the same slice (grouped runs, separator on >60min fixture, no per-bubble time); axe green both themes. **(W0.10a)**

## P-MSG2.2 Avatars + thread identity
- [x] Inbox rows load real avatars: `Avatar src={userAvatarUrl(other_user_id)}` (404 → initials); adopt `other_has_avatar` to skip probes when the regenerated client exposes it (needs core D3 — degrade fine without). **(W0.10a)**
- [x] Thread: 28px other-party avatar once per run, bottom-aligned in a reserved gutter; own runs no avatar; `ThreadHeader` with back chevron, 32px avatar, name + @username line, lock pill on encrypted, Report affordance replacing the always-visible per-message links footprint. **(W0.10a — deviations: back chevron is shown at ALL breakpoints (not `<lg` only) since the split-pane rail lands in P-MSG2.7; the header action is a direct "Report user" affordance rather than a Dropdown overflow menu, and "View profile" is omitted — no user-profile route exists yet.)**
- [x] Mocked e2e: one-avatar-per-run assertion (`getByTestId("run-avatar")`), header roles/names; axe green. **(W0.10a)**

## P-MSG2.3 Scroll architecture + live-ness
- [x] Make the timeline its own scroll container (page no longer scrolls on /messages/[id]); open pinned to bottom pre-paint; auto-stick only within 120px of bottom; `behavior:"auto"` everywhere (reduced-motion safe). **(W0.10a)**
- [ ] Scroll-to-bottom pill (>300px from bottom; shows "N new" when messages arrive while scrolled up; labelled button) + NEW divider from unread watermark + "Load earlier" prepend with scroll anchoring (offset paging now; switch to `before_id` when core D2 lands). **(W0.10a: pill + NEW divider DONE (divider anchored via a contract-free `?unread` hint on the inbox row link, since the thread has no viewer-watermark endpoint yet); "Load earlier"/history prepend DEFERRED to part 2 pending keyset `before_id` — core D2 — offset paging is incorrect under concurrent sends.)**
- [x] Poll thread page 1 every 10s (visible tab only, abortable, merge-by-id) and inbox every 30s + on focus; mark-read only when at bottom AND focused; offline banner after 2 failed polls with `role="status"`. **(W0.10a)**
- [ ] BACKED (DB-proof): second browser context receives a sent message within one poll interval WITHOUT reload; assert recipient UI + API read-back; read receipt propagates to sender's poll ("Seen"). (Evidence: `e2e-backed/messaging.spec.ts` extended, trace + API read-back captured.) **(Part 2 — needs the docker-backed stack.)**

## P-MSG2.4 Sending states + message actions
- [x] Optimistic send: pending bubble (60% opacity, "Sending…"), replaced by server Message on 201; composer no longer hard-locks during send. **(W0.10a)**
- [x] Failed send: "Not sent · Retry" (`text-danger`, `role="alert"`) with Retry (re-post same body/attachment_ids) and Discard; mocked e2e covers 500→retry→success. **(W0.10a)**
- [x] "Seen" rendered ONLY under the last own message covered by `peer_last_read_message_id`; hover/focus-revealed per-message actions (Delete own / Report peer) replace always-visible links; coarse-pointer fallback always-visible at reduced opacity. **(W0.10a — deviation: actions are hover/focus-revealed inline buttons, NOT yet consolidated into a Dropdown overflow with "Copy text" + padded 44px hit targets; that consolidation lands in part 2.)**
- [ ] Read receipts ON by default (decided 2026-07-07): Settings → Privacy "Read receipts" Toggle (default on) wired to `GET/PATCH /api/v1/me/messaging-prefs`, reciprocity copy ("…you won't see when they read yours"), local-off also suppresses rendering the peer's Seen; optimistic toggle with rollback; mocked e2e for default-on + PATCH + rollback. **(Part 2 — Settings surface, out of the thread-view core.)**
- [ ] BACKED (DB-proof): retry-after-abort persists (API read-back proves the row); delete tombstone + report unchanged and still green; prefs toggle off → peer's Seen disappears (settings + thread round trip). (Evidence: backed spec + trace.) **(Part 2 — needs the docker-backed stack.)**

> **### W0.10a evidence — Messaging v2 part 1 of 2 (thread-view core) — DONE 2026-07-07.**
> **What shipped:** the bare `/messages/[id]` thread became a proper messenger. New pure lib
> `lib/messaging/{grouping,time}.ts` (`buildTimeline` → separators/NEW-divider/runs; 24h-local
> separator labels + absolute-time formatter) with 21 vitest cases (run boundaries, sender change,
> 5-min/60-min thresholds, day/year label matrix, Seen skipping a pending copy, NEW-divider split,
> tombstone-in-run, empty). New components `components/messaging/{MessageTimeline,MessageGroup,
> MessageBubble,ThreadHeader,TimeSeparator}.tsx`; `ConversationView.tsx` rewired to render them.
> Thread now: message-run grouping (same sender ≤5 min), day/gap separators replacing per-bubble
> timestamps (hidden `<time datetime>` + `title` + accessible-name prefix keep the exact time),
> position-dependent bubble radii, other-party 28px avatar once per run (bottom-aligned gutter),
> quiet "Seen" under the last own read message (from the existing read watermark — no invented
> delivered tick), sticky `ThreadHeader` (back chevron + 32px avatar + name/@username + lock),
> its own scroll container (page no longer scrolls; pinned-to-bottom pre-paint; 120px auto-stick;
> `behavior:"auto"`), scroll-to-bottom "N new" pill, NEW-messages divider (from the inbox `?unread`
> hint), optimistic send (pending 60%-opacity bubble → server message on 201) with "Not sent ·
> Retry/Discard" on failure, 10s scroll-preserving merge-by-id polling (+ 30s inbox polling on
> interval/focus), mark-read only at-bottom-and-focused, and a `role="status"` offline banner after
> 2 failed polls. Inbox rows now load real avatars (`userAvatarUrl`, 404→initials). Per-message
> Delete/Report moved to hover/focus reveal (coarse-pointer always-on). Encrypted branch preserved
> (rendered under the same header in a scroll wrapper). App-shell `#main-content` got `min-h-0` so
> the thread pane can own its scroll.
> **Evidence:** BEFORE/AFTER screenshots (light+dark × 390/1440) in
> `.ralph/design-review/w0/{messaging,messaging-inbox}/` (git-ignored; `before/` subdir) via two new

> +run-avatar count; header name/@handle/back/report; optimistic 500→Retry→success; NEW divider from
> `?unread`) — 18/18 in isolation; new thread-route axe test in `e2e/a11y.spec.ts`; `e2ee` 7/7,
> `responsive`+`a11y` messages green. **Gate:** `npm run ci` ran to completion — typecheck ✓ lint ✓
> unit (32 files) ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e **369/373**; the 4 fails are the documented
> full-parallel flakes (`admin-overview` ×3 + `quarantine.spec.ts:255` NotificationsBell popover),

> the split-pane shell (P-MSG2.7) will pass it through instead. **Part 2 (leaves W0.10 open):** composer
> v2 (P-MSG2.5), media grids/lightbox (P-MSG2.6), desktop split-pane + rail (P-MSG2.7), Settings
> read-receipts toggle + per-message overflow/Copy-text consolidation + "Load earlier", backed

> **### W0.10b evidence — Messaging v2 part 2 of 2 (inbox split-pane, composer, media, notifications) — DONE 2026-07-07.**
> **What shipped:** the messenger became a proper split-pane app. New `components/messaging/`:
> `MessagingShell` (shared Next `layout` over `/messages` + `/messages/[id]` — persistent rail, single

Keyboard via a pure `shouldSendOnEnter` helper (`lib/messaging/composer-keys.ts`, unit-tested); fine-vs-coarse pointer from a SSR-safe `useSyncExternalStore` `matchMedia("(pointer: fine)")` hook; `e.nativeEvent.isComposing` guards IME; `aria-keyshortcuts="Enter"` on the send button when active.)**

Screenshots: `messaging` area (composer visible in the thread pane, light+dark × mobile+desktop).)**

## P-MSG2.6 Media v2 (attachments UX)
- [x] Multi-image messages: 2-col grid in one `rounded-2xl` mask (3rd spans full width), `aspect-square object-cover` cells; 5+ images → first 4 cells with `+N` overlay on the 4th opening the lightbox strip; single image `max-h-[320px] rounded-2xl`; media-only messages render without bubble fill. **(W0.10b — `components/messaging/MessageMedia.tsx`: single → `AttachmentImage` (`max-h-[320px] rounded-2xl`, click → lightbox); 2–4 → `grid grid-cols-2 gap-0.5` in one `rounded-2xl overflow-hidden` mask (3-image first cell `col-span-2`, cells `aspect-square object-cover`); 5+ → first four cells with a `bg-black/55 +N` overlay on the fourth opening the paged lightbox. `MessageAttachments` now splits image kinds → `MessageMedia` from file kinds → download rows; a media-only message (no body) already drops the bubble chrome in `MessageBubble`.)**
- [x] Intrinsic-ratio boxes from `DMAttachment.width/height` once core D1 lands (needs core; until then keep fixed skeleton); `MessageLightbox` on the Modal primitive (natural size, filename+size caption, Download, focus trap). **(W0.10b — `MessageLightbox` on the Modal a11y contract: image at natural size `max-h-[85dvh]`, `filename · size [· i of n]` caption, Download (auth blob → object-URL save), paged prev/next, Escape/scrim close + focus restore. **Dependency D1:** `DMAttachment.width/height` are NOT in the regenerated client, so grid cells use `object-cover` and single images `object-contain` (no reserved intrinsic aspect box yet) — degrades cleanly; will reserve `aspect-ratio: w/h` when D1 lands.)**
- [x] Upload chips: XHR-based progress %, image thumbnail previews from local File, failed chip Retry (kept File) + Remove; send never blocked by a failed optional upload. **(W0.10b — `api.uploadDMAttachment` gained an optional `{ onProgress, signal }` that routes through the shared XHR `uploadWithProgress` (fetch can't observe upload progress); the no-progress path stays on the fetch client so `endpoints.test.ts` is unchanged. Composer chips: 40px local-`File` `URL.createObjectURL` thumbnail for images (revoked on remove/unmount), a determinate progress bar + `%`, and an error chip with Retry (re-uploads the kept `File`) + Remove. `canSend` ignores errored/removed chips, so a failed optional upload never blocks Send.)**
- [ ] Adopt Messenger-parity limits when core D6 lands (needs core): `MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES` → 100 MiB, `MAX_ATTACHMENTS` → 30, accept list + `isAllowedAttachment` gain office-doc MIME types, `doc` kind renders via `AttachmentDownloadRow` (new glyph), 413 copy updated; until then current 25 MiB / 4 constants stay. **(BLOCKED on core D6 — `DMAttachment.kind` has no `doc` value and no raised limits in the regenerated client; the current 25 MiB / 4 / image·video·audio·pdf constants stay per the spec's degrade rule.)**

# Design refresh (user-supplied "Vidra streaming platform design")

Component restyle + new-surface wave adopting the user's design across vidra-user.
Authoritative package: `.ralph/specs/design-refresh.md` (gap spec) + `.ralph/specs/design-refresh-icons.md`
(41-icon path inventory) + `.ralph/design-templates/*.dc.html` (vendored design source).
HARD RULES: **zero token-value changes** (the 4 light-mode deltas fail WCAG AA — current
accessible values stay); SVG icons only, never emoji/glyphs; no `dark:` variants; no raw
palette classes (black shadows/scrims are the sanctioned theme-invariant exception); axe
gate stays green; aspirational items (spec §5.6) are listed, never stubbed. Every slice:
before/after screenshots (light+dark, 390px+1280px) via `npm run design:shots`, full
`npm run ci` green, push.

## DR1 — Foundation: primitives + icon set (no page-level visual churn)

      from the inventory), `strokeWidth` prop (default 1.8) + `size`, filled variants
      (Play/Playlist/MoreHorizontal/library glyph), re-pathed existing icons (Flag/Bell/
      Search/Check/chevrons/Info/Warning), `e2ee/LockIcon` folded in, IPFS cube, MIT header;
      unit test render + a11y contract. **DONE 2026-07-07.** Set is now the single icon
      source: added Home/PlusSquare/TrendingUp/Tv/Clock/Library/Video/MessageCircle/Lock/

      RotateCw/Copy/User/Shield/SlashCircle/Grid/Users/Server/Ipfs (+ re-pathed the 14
      originals to design `d`s). Default stroke 1.8 (design standard). Filled glyphs set
      `fill=currentColor stroke=none` per-path (Library keeps a stroked outer rect + filled
      inner play). IPFS cube is the sole non-24 viewBox (12×14, sw1.4). `e2ee/LockIcon.tsx`
      DELETED; its 6 consumers (DevicesView, StartEncryptedButton, DeviceSetup,
      EncryptedThreadView, ConversationRail, ThreadHeader) re-import the shared `LockIcon`
      with explicit `size` (preserves the 12/14/16px renders). MIT (Feather) attribution in
      the header. `components/icons/index.test.tsx` (8 cases: decorative default, labeled →

      `danger-outline` = `border border-danger/45 bg-transparent text-danger hover:bg-danger/10`
      (uses the AA-safe `danger` text token, not the solid fill). Existing 4 variants

- [x] `ui/Badge.tsx`: uppercase micro `status` style + role pills (ADMIN inverse, MOD strong).

      (`items-end`), `rounded-t-[22px] px-5 pt-3 pb-[max(env(safe-area-inset-bottom),2.75rem)]`
      + a 36×4 decorative `bg-border` grab handle. **Kept `max-w-md`** (≈448px, within 8px of
      the design's 440px) instead of `max-w-[440px]` so MessageLightbox's `max-w-3xl` override
      keeps winning (no tailwind-merge on plain `cn`). Focus-trap/Escape/scrim/close a11y

- [x] Bookkeeping: specs copied into `.ralph/specs/`, this section appended; harden

## DR2 — Icon sweep: kill all emoji/glyph icons (5 call sites)
- [x] `RatingControls` 👍/👎 → ThumbsUp/ThumbsDown; `ReportButton` ⚑ → FlagIcon; `SaveButton`
      ★/☆ → the design's plus+"Save" pill; `PlayerMenu` ✓ + `AddToPlaylistButton` ✓ → CheckIcon.
      Do NOT convert `·`/`…`/`×`/`←→` keycaps/`—`/LIVE dot. **DONE 2026-07-07.** A tree-wide
      codepoint scan (components/ app/ e2e/ lib/) found the documented 5 sites PLUS one the
      spec's audit missed: a stray fullwidth plus `＋` (U+FF0B) at `AddToPlaylistButton.tsx:93`
      — killed too (→ `PlusIcon`). Six sites, all now SVG:
      • `RatingControls.tsx` 👍/👎 → `ThumbsUpIcon`/`ThumbsDownIcon`. The design source has NO
        like/dislike surface (its watch actions are Support/Share/Download/Save/Report), so these
        two icons are vendored from **Feather (MIT, already credited in the icon header)** rather
        than the design files — feather-style single-path draws, set's 1.8 stroke, round joins.
      • `ReportButton.tsx` ⚑ → `FlagIcon` (the pennant re-pathed in DR1).
      • `SaveButton.tsx` ★/☆ → **the design's plus+"Save" pill** (settled decision; supersedes the
        old "→ StarIcon, revisit in DR5" note — no StarIcon created). The pill was already tonal
        rounded-full (matches the design's `--sm` 99px Save pill); only the glyph changed:
        `PlusIcon` unsaved / `CheckIcon` saved (accent-filled + aria-pressed still carry state).
      • `PlayerMenu.tsx` ✓ → `CheckIcon` in a `flex w-4 justify-center` slot — covers Speed AND
        Quality menus (both delegate to PlayerMenu).
      • `AddToPlaylistButton.tsx` ＋ (:93) → `PlusIcon`; ✓ (:114) → `CheckIcon`.
      Every icon stays decorative (`aria-hidden`, no `label`) — the buttons already own the
      accessible name (`aria-label`/text), so e2e selectors ("Like"/"Dislike"/"Save"/"Saved") are
      unchanged and green. Cleaned a now-vestigial `.replace("✓","")` in
      `VideoPlayer.test.tsx:119` (the selected speed's check is an SVG → no text content).
      **Scope (spec §7.2):** the ~45 ad-hoc inline `<svg>` components (ShareButton, DownloadButton,
      DonateButton, …) are already SVG, not emoji — they converge onto the shared set
      *per-surface* in their restyle slices (DR5 watch-actions, …) and the DR13 audit, NOT a
      big-bang here. DR2 = emoji/glyph kill + regression guard only.
      Icon set: added `ThumbsUpIcon`/`ThumbsDownIcon` to `components/icons` (+2 unit cases).
- [x] CI guard: grep/lint failing on emoji codepoints in `components/ app/` JSX. **DONE
      2026-07-07.** New `scripts/check-no-emoji.mjs` (importable `findEmojiViolations()` + CLI,
      styled on `check-contract.mjs`) scans components/ and app/ for banned codepoint RANGES —
      Misc Symbols U+2600–26FF (★☆⚑), Dingbats U+2700–27BF (✓), Misc Symbols & Arrows U+2B00–2BFF,
      the emoji planes U+1F000–1FAFF (👍👎), variation selectors U+FE00–0F, ZWJ, and fullwidth
      ASCII forms U+FF01–5E (＋). Deliberately does NOT ban the design's text typography (· … ×
      ← → — – curly quotes all fall outside those ranges). Wired into `npm run ci` as
      `lint:icons` (new script) before the unit run, AND mirrored as a vitest unit test
      (`components/icons/no-emoji.test.tsx`): asserts the source is clean, that the detector
      actually catches a planted emoji/dingbat/star/fullwidth (built via `String.fromCodePoint`
      so the test file itself stays glyph-free — the guard scans tests too), and that the allowed
      typography is not flagged.
- [x] Evidence + `npm run ci` + push. **DONE 2026-07-07.** Before/after `watch` + `watch-speed`
      (light+dark, 390/1280) in `.ralph/design-review/dr2/{before,after}/` (git-ignored). Read
      after-PNGs vs the design: the watch action row now reads as clean monochrome feather glyphs
      (thumbs-up/down, `+ Save`, `+ Save to playlist`, pennant Report) inheriting `currentColor` —
      before it was colored emoji (yellow 👍👎) clashing with the monochrome system; the speed
      menu's selected `1×` shows a feather check (was `✓`). Full `npm run ci` GREEN: typecheck,
      lint, lint:icons, 564 unit (48 files), build, e2e 397/398 — the 1 failure
      (`quarantine.spec.ts:255`, a video_rejected notification bell flow, touches none of the
      swapped components) is a full-parallel flake: passes in isolation and on a scoped re-run
      with rating/save specs (11/11). Pushed.

## DR3 — Shell: BottomTabBar + Header + Sidebar + Create sheet
- [x] Tab/sidebar/header icons → design paths; Create tab opens bottom sheet (Upload / Go
      live / Open Studio); sidebar FOLLOWING pulsing live dot; row radius 9px. **DONE
      2026-07-07.** The three shell surfaces now render iconography from the shared
      `components/icons` set (DR1's single source) instead of ad-hoc inline `<svg>`s, and
      the Create tab opens a sheet instead of navigating.
      • **Icons.** The **tab bar** glyphs were already the design paths (W0.3) — converted
        the inline `tabIcon()` SVGs to `<HomeIcon/SearchIcon/PlusSquareIcon/BellIcon/
        LibraryIcon>` components (Create = `PlusSquareIcon size=30 sw=1.5`, the design's
        rounded plus-square). The **sidebar** was the real gap: it drew the OLD
        `nav-links` single-path strings (RSS-style Subscriptions, bookmark Library,
        video-camera Studio). Refactored `components/nav-links.ts` to carry an `Icon`
        component ref per entry (Home→`HomeIcon`, Trending→`TrendingUpIcon`,
        Subscriptions→`TvIcon`, Library→`LibraryIcon`, History→`ClockIcon`,
        Messages→`MessageCircleIcon`, Studio→`VideoIcon`; Moderation→`ShieldIcon`,
        Admin→`UsersIcon`; About→`InfoIcon`) so `Sidebar` renders the exact design glyphs
        at 18px sw1.9. `nav-links` has exactly one consumer (`Sidebar`), so the shape
        change is contained. The **header** desktop "+ Create" pill's inline plus →
        `PlusIcon size=14 sw=2.2` (outlined-pill style kept).
      • **Create sheet (new surface).** `components/CreateSheet.tsx` on the shared Modal
        `sheet` skin (DR1 — grab handle, rounded-t-[22px], safe-area footer, backdrop +
        Escape dismiss, focus-trap + restore-to-opener). The Create tab is now a
        `<button aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-expanded>` (was a `<Link>` to /studio) that
        opens it; three rows — **Upload a video** (`/studio#upload`, `UploadIcon`,
        "Resumable, up to 8 GB") · **Go live** (`/studio#go-live`, red status dot [not a
        glyph, per the design], "Stream via RTMP with optional replay") · **Open Studio**
        (`/studio`, `VideoIcon`, "Manage videos, streams and captions") — each a `<Link>`
        in a 40px `rounded-xl bg-surface-muted` tile that calls `onClose` on navigate.
        `hideClose` (the design shows no X). Added `id="upload"`/`id="go-live"` +
        `scroll-mt-20` anchors around the Upload/Live sections in `StudioView` so the rows
        land in place (the app consolidates upload + go-live onto `/studio`; no standalone
        `/upload` or `/go-live` route exists).
      • **Row radius 9px.** Sidebar nav rows, About, Collapse, and `SidebarFollowing` rows:
        `rounded-lg` (8px) → `rounded-[9px]` (design).
      • **FOLLOWING pulsing live dot — NOT built (dependency recorded, not stubbed).**
        Re-verified the contract FRESH against `vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml` at execution
        time: the `/me/subscriptions` payload is `FollowedChannel = Channel & {followed_at}`
        and `Channel` carries **no `is_live`/unread field** — there is no per-followed-
        channel live signal anywhere. Per the truthfulness rule the dot stays deferred
        (matches the existing `SidebarFollowing` W0.2 note); the buildable FOLLOWING delta
        (row radius) shipped. **Backend dependency:** a live/unread flag on the followed-
        channel list item.
      • **Tests (unit + e2e, same slice).** New `components/CreateSheet.test.tsx` (4:
        renders null when closed; dialog "Create" + the 3 rows with correct hrefs;
        dismiss-on-navigate; Escape closes). Updated `e2e/mobile-nav.spec.ts`: Create is now
        asserted as a **button** (not a link); split the old routing test into "Library tab
        routes" + a new "Create tab opens the sheet, whose rows lead into the studio"
        (asserts `aria-expanded` false→true, Upload/Go live rows visible, Open Studio →
        `/studio`). `sidebar.spec`/`a11y-landmarks` are label/role-based → unaffected by the
        icon swap and stay green. Extended `scripts/design-shots.mjs` with a `create-sheet`
        area (opens the sheet via an act hook, guarded so the desktop capture — where the
        bottom bar is hidden — is just the feed).
      • **Deviation:** the header bell keeps its numeric unread-count badge (the W0.3-
        accepted divergence from the mockup's plain red dot — more information, sanctioned
        by design-system.md) — not regressed here.

## DR4 — Home/feed + Live rail (mobile + desktop)
- [x] Mobile single-column cards (16px thumbs, IPFS cube chip top-left, duration chip, 36px
      avatar rows); desktop 3-col grid (IPFS top-right); "Live now" rail (240px cards, LIVE

      **DONE 2026-07-07.** Built the "Live now" rail — the W0 audit's one remaining major —
      bound to the REAL public live-listing contract, plus the card-level home/feed deltas.
      • **The contract now EXISTS (checked fresh).** vidra-core's working tree carries the
        W1.C4 public live listing: `GET /api/v1/live → LivePublicListResponse { live_streams:
        LiveStreamCard[], limit, offset }`, `LiveStreamCard = { id, title, description?,
        channel_handle, channel_display_name, started_at?, is_live, hls_url? }`. Deliberately
        NO viewer/concurrent count and NO thumbnail (the spec says both are W4 dependencies,
        omitted not faked) — which lines up exactly with the aspirational-omissions list (§5.6):
        the design's "N watching" chip and any live poster are NOT rendered.
      • **Types regenerated the repo's way.** `npm run codegen` (openapi-typescript over
        `../vidra-core/api/openapi.yaml`) refreshed `lib/api/generated.ts` — this also caught up
        accumulated backend drift the frontend hadn't regenerated for (W1.C1 chapters, **W1.C2
        video passwords**, W1.C3 player settings). Added `LiveStreamCard`/`LivePublicListResponse`
        aliases to `lib/api/types.ts` and an `api.listLivePublicStreams({limit,offset}, signal)`
        wrapper to `endpoints.ts`. **Incidental drift fixed (2 sites, from the mandated regen,
        NOT new feature work):** the new `privacy: "password"` enum made two components
        non-exhaustive — `PrivacyBadge.tsx` (added the "password" case: warning-tinted lock +
        "Anyone with the password can watch this") and `LiveStreamsSection.tsx` (narrowed the
        live-create privacy state to `NonNullable<CreateLiveStreamRequest["privacy"]>` =
        public/unlisted/private, since live streams have no password mode).
      • **New `components/LiveNowRail.tsx`** — client component fetching `GET /api/v1/live` on
        mount; renders NOTHING while loading, on error, or when nothing is live (a quiet
        progressive-enhancement surface, exactly like `ChannelLiveBadge`/`SidebarFollowing` —
        never reserves space, never shows an error UI on a public feed). A named `<section
        aria-label="Live now">` region with an `<h2>` + pluralized "N stream(s)" count; a
        horizontal `overflow-x-auto` rail of 240px `<li>` cards, each a single `<Link>` to
        `/live/{id}` with a clean aria-label. Card = `aspect-video rounded-[14px]
        bg-surface-muted` tile carrying the media-overlay LIVE pill (dark black/55 blur pill +
        pulsing `bg-live` dot, the theme-invariant sanctioned exception; pulse globally
        neutralized under prefers-reduced-motion) + a faint decorative `PlayIcon` in
        `text-fg-subtle` (poster-less live streams have no server thumbnail — an honest "watchable
        video" affordance, never a fabricated image), then title (14px) + channel (12.5px muted).
        Mounted on BOTH `app/page.tsx` and `app/trending/page.tsx` (the design's single feed
        screen keeps the rail across the Recent/Popular/Trending re-sorts; our split routes each
        get it for consistency).
      • **VideoCard deltas (§3.3).** (1) IPFS chip repositioned responsively — **top-left on
        mobile, top-right on desktop** (`left-2 top-2 lg:left-auto lg:right-2`), per the app-vs-
        desktop templates; (2) **converged the ad-hoc inline hexagon SVG onto the shared
        `IpfsIcon`** cube (DR1's single icon source; the design's 10×11 cube), shown on mobile,
        `lg:hidden` on desktop (desktop chip is text-only, per the desktop template), design
        opacity/tracking (`bg-black/50 text-white/90 text-[10.5px] tracking-[0.03em]`); (3) title
        bumped to the design's **15.5px on mobile**, kept 14px on desktop (`text-[15.5px]
        lg:text-sm`) — both breakpoints now match. Meta (13px) + 36px avatar already matched.
        The `title="Mirrored to IPFS"` hook is unchanged (home.spec IPFS assertion stays green).
        **LIVE-on-feed-cards stays omitted** (live streams are a table disjoint from videos — the

      • **Tests (unit + e2e, same slice).** New `components/LiveNowRail.test.tsx` (4: renders
        nothing when nothing's live; renders nothing on a failed read; a card per stream with the

        abort signal). New `e2e/live-rail.spec.ts` (3: home rail with 2 cards + region/heading/

        links, no error). Extended `scripts/design-shots.mjs` (home+trending mock `/api/v1/live`
        with a 2-stream `SAMPLE_LIVE`).

        slice's changes) + after (`dr4/after/{home,trending}/*`), light+dark × mobile+desktop,

        matches ("Live now" + "N streams", 240px LIVE-badged tiles with pulse dot, no watching

        with the cube; feed titles read 15.5/14; dark inverts via tokens with the LIVE/IPFS
        media pills correctly theme-invariant. No horizontal overflow at 390.
      • **Gate.** Full `CI=1 npm run ci` GREEN on the final tree — typecheck ✓ lint ✓ lint:icons ✓
        unit **572/572** (50 files, +4 LiveNowRail) ✓ `next build` ✓ mocked e2e **402/402
        (0 failed, no flakes)** on the first full run (+3 live-rail). Token grep clean on changed
        files: only semantic tokens (`bg-surface-muted`/`bg-live`/`text-fg`/`text-fg-muted`/
        `text-fg-subtle`/`text-warning`) + the sanctioned media-overlay `bg-black/*` + `text-white`
        on the LIVE/IPFS pills; no raw palette/hex/`dark:`.
      • **Cross-repo dependency (recorded, honest).** The `/api/v1/live` listing is in vidra-core's
